## Copyright Act {#copyright-act .Title-of-Act}

[R.S.C.]{.abbr title="Revised Statutes of Canada"}, 1985, c. C-42
:::

An Act respecting copyright
::::

::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
## [Short Title]{.HTitleText1} {#h-102546 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Short title

**[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** This Act may be cited as
the [Copyright Act](/eng/acts/C-42).

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. C-30, s. 1
:::

## [Interpretation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-102551 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** In this Act,

[[architectural work]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[architectural work]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any building or
    structure or any model of a building or structure; ([oeuvre
    architecturale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[architectural work of art]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[architectural work of art]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
    1993, c. 44, s. 53\]]{.Repealed}

[[artistic work]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[artistic work]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes paintings, drawings,
    maps, charts, plans, photographs, engravings, sculptures, works of
    artistic craftsmanship, architectural works, and compilations of
    artistic works; ([oeuvre artistique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Berne Convention country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Berne Convention country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a country
    that is a party to the Convention for the Protection of Literary and
    Artistic Works concluded at Berne on September 9, 1886, or any one
    of its revisions, including the Paris Act of 1971; ([pays partie à
    la Convention de Berne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Board]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Board]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Copyright Board established
    by subsection 66(1); ([Commission]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[book]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[book]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a volume or a part or division of
    a volume, in printed form, but does not include

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a pamphlet,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a newspaper, review, magazine or other
      periodical,

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a map, chart, plan or sheet music where the map,
      chart, plan or sheet music is separately published, and

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an instruction or repair manual that accompanies
      a product or that is supplied as an accessory to a
      service; ([livre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[broadcaster]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[broadcaster]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a body that, in the course
    of operating a broadcasting undertaking, broadcasts a communication
    signal in accordance with the law of the country in which the
    broadcasting undertaking is carried on, but excludes a body whose
    primary activity in relation to communication signals is their
    retransmission; ([radiodiffuseur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Canada--United States--Mexico Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Canada--United States--Mexico Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has
    the meaning assigned by the definition
    [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 of the
    [Canada--United States--Mexico Agreement Implementation
    Act](/eng/acts/C-10.65); ([Accord
    Canada--États-Unis--Mexique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[choreographic work]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[choreographic work]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes any work of
    choreography, whether or not it has any story line; ([oeuvre
    chorégraphique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[cinematograph]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[cinematograph]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 1997, c. 24, s.
    1\]]{.Repealed}

[[cinematographic work]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[cinematographic work]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes any work
    expressed by any process analogous to cinematography, whether or not
    accompanied by a soundtrack; ([oeuvre
    cinématographique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[collective society]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[collective society]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a society,
    association or corporation that carries on the business of
    collective administration of copyright or of the remuneration right
    conferred by section 19 or 81 for the benefit of those who, by
    assignment, grant of licence, appointment of it as their agent or
    otherwise, authorize it to act on their behalf in relation to that
    collective administration, and

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} operates a licensing scheme, applicable in
      relation to a repertoire of works, performer's performances, sound
      recordings or communication signals of more than one author,
      performer, sound recording maker or broadcaster, pursuant to which
      the society, association or corporation sets out classes of uses
      that it agrees to authorize under this Act, and the royalties and
      terms and conditions on which it agrees to authorize those classes
      of uses, or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} carries on the business of collecting and
      distributing royalties or levies payable under this Act in
      relation to a repertoire of works, performer's performances, sound
      recordings or communication signals of more than one author,
      performer, sound recording maker or broadcaster; ([société de
      gestion]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[collective work]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[collective work]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an encyclopaedia, dictionary, year book or
      similar work,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a newspaper, review, magazine or similar
      periodical, and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any work written in distinct parts by different
      authors, or in which works or parts of works of different authors
      are incorporated; ([recueil]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[commercially available]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[commercially available]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in relation to
    a work or other subject-matter,

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} available on the Canadian market within a
      reasonable time and for a reasonable price and may be located with
      reasonable effort, or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for which a licence to reproduce, perform in
      public or communicate to the public by telecommunication is
      available from a collective society within a reasonable time and
      for a reasonable price and may be located with reasonable
      effort; ([accessible sur le marché]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[communication signal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[communication signal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means radio waves
    transmitted through space without any artificial guide, for
    reception by the public; ([signal de communication]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[compilation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[compilation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a work resulting from the selection or
      arrangement of literary, dramatic, musical or artistic works or of
      parts thereof, or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a work resulting from the selection or
      arrangement of data; ([compilation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[computer program]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[computer program]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a set of instructions
    or statements, expressed, fixed, embodied or stored in any manner,
    that is to be used directly or indirectly in a computer in order to
    bring about a specific result; ([programme
    d'ordinateur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[copyright]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[copyright]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the rights described in

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} section 3, in the case of a work,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} sections 15 and 26, in the case of a performer's
      performance,

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} section 18, in the case of a sound recording, or

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} section 21, in the case of a communication
      signal; ([droit d'auteur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes any
    territory; ([pays]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[defendant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[defendant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a respondent to an
    application; ([Version anglaise
    seulement]{.DefinitionOtherLangOnly})

[[delivery]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[delivery]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 1997, c. 24, s.
    1\]]{.Repealed}

[[dramatic work]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[dramatic work]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any piece for recitation, choreographic work or
      mime, the scenic arrangement or acting form of which is fixed in
      writing or otherwise,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any cinematographic work, and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any compilation of dramatic works; ([oeuvre
      dramatique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[educational institution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[educational institution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a non-profit institution licensed or recognized
      by or under an Act of Parliament or the legislature of a province
      to provide pre-school, elementary, secondary or post-secondary
      education,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a non-profit institution that is directed or
      controlled by a board of education regulated by or under an Act of
      the legislature of a province and that provides continuing,
      professional or vocational education or training,

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a department or agency of any order of
      government, or any non-profit body, that controls or supervises
      education or training referred to in paragraph (a) or (b), or

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any other non-profit institution prescribed by
      regulation; ([établissement d'enseignement]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

[[engravings]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[engravings]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes etchings, lithographs,
    woodcuts, prints and other similar works, not being
    photographs; ([gravure]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[every original literary, dramatic, musical and artistic work]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[every original literary, dramatic, musical and artistic
    work]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes every original production in the
    literary, scientific or artistic domain, whatever may be the mode or
    form of its expression, such as compilations, books, pamphlets and
    other writings, lectures, dramatic or dramatico-musical works,
    musical works, translations, illustrations, sketches and plastic
    works relative to geography, topography, architecture or
    science; ([toute oeuvre littéraire, dramatique, musicale ou
    artistique originale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[exclusive distributor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[exclusive distributor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in relation to
    a book, a person who

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has, before or after the coming into force of
      this definition, been appointed in writing, by the owner or
      exclusive licensee of the copyright in the book in Canada, as

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the only distributor of the book in Canada or
        any part of Canada, or

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the only distributor of the book in Canada or
        any part of Canada in respect of a particular sector of the
        market, and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} meets the criteria established by regulations
      made under section 2.6,

    and, for greater certainty, if there are no regulations made under
    section 2.6, then no person qualifies under this definition as an
    "exclusive distributor"; ([distributeur exclusif]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[Her Majesty's Realms and Territories]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Her Majesty's Realms and
    Territories]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 1997, c. 24, s.
    1\]]{.Repealed}

[[infringing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[infringing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in relation to a work in which copyright
      subsists, any copy, including any colourable imitation, made or
      dealt with in contravention of this Act,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in relation to a performer's performance in
      respect of which copyright subsists, any fixation or copy of a
      fixation of it made or dealt with in contravention of this Act,

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in relation to a sound recording in respect of
      which copyright subsists, any copy of it made or dealt with in
      contravention of this Act, or

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in relation to a communication signal in respect
      of which copyright subsists, any fixation or copy of a fixation of
      it made or dealt with in contravention of this Act.

    The definition includes a copy that is imported in the circumstances
    set out in paragraph 27(2)(e) and section 27.1 but does not
    otherwise include a copy made with the consent of the owner of the
    copyright in the country where the copy was
    made; ([contrefaçon]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[lecture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[lecture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes address, speech and
    sermon; ([conférence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[legal representatives]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[legal representatives]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes heirs,
    executors, administrators, successors and assigns, or agents or
    attorneys who are thereunto duly authorized in
    writing; ([représentants légaux]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[library, archive or museum]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[library, archive or museum]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an institution, whether or not incorporated, that
      is not established or conducted for profit or that does not form a
      part of, or is not administered or directly or indirectly
      controlled by, a body that is established or conducted for profit,
      in which is held and maintained a collection of documents and
      other materials that is open to the public or to researchers, or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any other non-profit institution prescribed by
      regulation; ([bibliothèque, musée ou service
      d'archives]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[literary work]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[literary work]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes tables, computer
    programs, and compilations of literary works; ([oeuvre
    littéraire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[maker]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[maker]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in relation to a cinematographic work, the person
      by whom the arrangements necessary for the making of the work are
      undertaken, or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in relation to a sound recording, the person by
      whom the arrangements necessary for the first fixation of the
      sounds are undertaken; ([producteur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, except in sections 44 to 44.12,
    means the Minister of Industry; ([ministre]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[moral rights]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[moral rights]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the rights described in
    subsections 14.1(1) and 17.1(1); ([droits moraux]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[musical work]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[musical work]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any work of music or
    musical composition, with or without words, and includes any
    compilation thereof; ([oeuvre musicale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[perceptual disability]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[perceptual disability]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a disability
    that prevents or inhibits a person from reading or hearing a
    literary, musical, dramatic or artistic work in its original format,
    and includes such a disability resulting from

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} severe or total impairment of sight or hearing or
      the inability to focus or move one's eyes,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the inability to hold or manipulate a book, or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an impairment relating to
      comprehension; ([déficience perceptuelle]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

[[performance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[performance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any acoustic or visual
    representation of a work, performer's performance, sound recording
    or communication signal, including a representation made by means of
    any mechanical instrument, radio receiving set or television
    receiving set; ([représentation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou
    [exécution]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[performer's performance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[performer's performance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any of the
    following when done by a performer:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a performance of an artistic work, dramatic work
      or musical work, whether or not the work was previously fixed in
      any material form, and whether or not the work's term of copyright
      protection under this Act has expired,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a recitation or reading of a literary work,
      whether or not the work's term of copyright protection under this
      Act has expired, or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an improvisation of a dramatic work, musical work
      or literary work, whether or not the improvised work is based on a
      pre-existing work; ([prestation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[photograph]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[photograph]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes photo-lithograph and any
    work expressed by any process analogous to
    photography; ([photographie]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[plaintiff]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[plaintiff]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes an applicant; ([Version
    anglaise seulement]{.DefinitionOtherLangOnly})

[[plate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[plate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any stereotype or other plate, stone, block,
      mould, matrix, transfer or negative used or intended to be used
      for printing or reproducing copies of any work, and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any matrix or other appliance used or intended to
      be used for making or reproducing sound recordings, performer's
      performances or communication signals; ([planche]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

[[premises]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[premises]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in relation to an
    educational institution, a place where education or training
    referred to in the definition "educational institution" is provided,
    controlled or supervised by the educational
    institution; ([locaux]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[receiving device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[receiving device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 1993, c. 44, s.
    79\]]{.Repealed}

[[Rome Convention country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Rome Convention country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a country that
    is a party to the International Convention for the Protection of
    Performers, Producers of Phonograms and Broadcasting Organisations,
    done at Rome on October 26, 1961; ([pays partie à la Convention de
    Rome]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[sculpture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[sculpture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a cast or
    model; ([sculpture]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[sound recording]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[sound recording]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a recording, fixed in
    any material form, consisting of sounds, whether or not of a
    performance of a work, but excludes any soundtrack of a
    cinematographic work where it accompanies the cinematographic
    work; ([enregistrement sonore]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[telecommunication]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[telecommunication]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any transmission of
    signs, signals, writing, images or sounds or intelligence of any
    nature by wire, radio, visual, optical or other electromagnetic
    system; ([télécommunication]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[treaty country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[treaty country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a Berne Convention
    country, UCC country, WCT country or WTO Member; ([pays
    signataire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[UCC country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[UCC country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a country that is a party
    to the Universal Copyright Convention, adopted on September 6, 1952
    in Geneva, Switzerland, or to that Convention as revised in Paris,
    France on July 24, 1971; ([pays partie à la Convention
    universelle]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[WCT country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[WCT country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a country that is a party
    to the WIPO Copyright Treaty, adopted in Geneva on December 20,
    1996; ([pays partie au traité de l'ODA]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[work]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[work]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes the title thereof when such
    title is original and distinctive; ([oeuvre]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[work of joint authorship]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[work of joint authorship]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a work
    produced by the collaboration of two or more authors in which the
    contribution of one author is not distinct from the contribution of
    the other author or authors; ([oeuvre créée en
    collaboration]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[work of sculpture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[work of sculpture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 1997, c.
    24, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

[[WPPT country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[WPPT country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a country that is a party
    to the WIPO Performances and Phonograms Treaty, adopted in Geneva on
    December 20, 1996; ([pays partie au traité de
    l'OIEP]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[WTO Member]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[WTO Member]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a Member of the World Trade
    Organization as defined in subsection 2(1) of the [World Trade
    Organization Agreement Implementation
    Act](/eng/acts/W-11.8). ([membre de l'OMC]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compilations

- **[[2.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  compilation containing two or more of the categories of literary,
  dramatic, musical or artistic works shall be deemed to be a
  compilation of the category making up the most substantial part of the
  compilation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The mere fact that a work is included in a
  compilation does not increase, decrease or otherwise affect the
  protection conferred by this Act in respect of the copyright in the
  work or the moral rights in respect of the work.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1993, c. 44, s. 54
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
[[maker]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

**[[2.11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.11 .sectionLabel}** For greater
certainty, the arrangements referred to in paragraph (b) of the
definition [[maker]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2, as that term is
used in section 19 and in the definition [[eligible
maker]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 79, include arrangements for
entering into contracts with performers, financial arrangements and
technical arrangements required for the first fixation of the sounds for
a sound recording.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 2
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
[[publication]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[2.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of this Act, [[publication]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in relation to works,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} making copies of a work available to the public,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the construction of an architectural work, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the incorporation of an artistic work into an
      architectural work, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in relation to sound recordings, making copies of a
    sound recording available to the public,

  but does not include

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the performance in public, or the communication to
    the public by telecommunication, of a literary, dramatic, musical or
    artistic work or a sound recording, or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the exhibition in public of an artistic work.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issue of photographs and engravings

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of subsection (1), the issue of
  photographs and engravings of sculptures and architectural works is
  not deemed to be publication of those works.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where no consent of copyright owner

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, other than in respect
  of infringement of copyright, a work or other subject-matter is not
  deemed to be published or performed in public or communicated to the
  public by telecommunication if that act is done without the consent of
  the owner of the copyright.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unpublished works

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where, in the case of an unpublished work, the making
  of the work is extended over a considerable period, the conditions of
  this Act conferring copyright are deemed to have been complied with if
  the author was, during any substantial part of that period, a subject
  or citizen of, or a person ordinarily resident in, a country to which
  this Act extends.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 2
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Telecommunication

**[[2.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.3 .sectionLabel}** A person who
communicates a work or other subject-matter to the public by
telecommunication does not by that act alone perform it in public, nor
by that act alone is deemed to authorize its performance in public.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 2
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Communication to the public by
telecommunication

- **[[2.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.4 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of communication to the public by telecommunication,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} persons who occupy apartments, hotel rooms or
    dwelling units situated in the same building are part of the public,
    and a communication intended to be received exclusively by such
    persons is a communication to the public;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a person whose only act in respect of the
    communication of a work or other subject-matter to the public
    consists of providing the means of telecommunication necessary for
    another person to so communicate the work or other subject-matter
    does not communicate that work or other subject-matter to the
    public; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} where a person, as part of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a network, within the meaning of the
      [Broadcasting Act](/eng/acts/B-9.01), whose operations result in
      the communication of works or other subject-matter to the public,
      or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any programming undertaking whose operations
      result in the communication of works or other subject-matter to
      the public,

    transmits by telecommunication a work or other subject-matter that
    is communicated to the public by another person who is not a
    retransmitter of a signal within the meaning of subsection 31(1),
    the transmission and communication of that work or other
    subject-matter by those persons constitute a single communication to
    the public for which those persons are jointly and severally liable.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Communication to the public by
  telecommunication

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, communication of a
  work or other subject-matter to the public by telecommunication
  includes making it available to the public by telecommunication in a
  way that allows a member of the public to have access to it from a
  place and at a time individually chosen by that member of the public.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations defining
  "programming undertaking" for the purpose of paragraph (1)(c).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A work is not communicated in the manner described in
  paragraph (1)(c) or 3(1)(f) where a signal carrying the work is
  retransmitted to a person who is a retransmitter within the meaning of
  subsection 31(1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 2
- 2002, c. 26, s. 1
- 2012, c. 20, s. 3
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-2.4-20030321.html "Link to previous version of section 2.4"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}What constitutes rental

- **[[2.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.5 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of paragraphs 3(1)(h) and (i), 15(1)(c) and 18(1)(c), an
  arrangement, whatever its form, constitutes a rental of a computer
  program or sound recording if, and only if,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is in substance a rental, having regard to all
    the circumstances; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is entered into with motive of gain in relation
    to the overall operations of the person who rents out the computer
    program or sound recording, as the case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Motive of gain

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of paragraph (1)(b), a person who
  rents out a computer program or sound recording with the intention of
  recovering no more than the costs, including overhead, associated with
  the rental operations does not by that act alone have a motive of gain
  in relation to the rental operations.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 2
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exclusive distributor

**[[2.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.6 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in Council
may make regulations establishing distribution criteria for the purpose
of paragraph (b) of the definition [[exclusive
distributor]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 2
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exclusive licence

**[[2.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.7 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
this Act, an exclusive licence is an authorization to do any act that is
subject to copyright to the exclusion of all others including the
copyright owner, whether the authorization is granted by the owner or an
exclusive licensee claiming under the owner.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 2
:::

## [PART I]{.HLabel1}[Copyright and Moral Rights in Works]{.HTitleText1} {#h-102725 .Part}

### [Copyright]{.HTitleText2} {#h-102726 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copyright in works

- **[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the
  purposes of this Act, [[copyright]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation
  to a work, means the sole right to produce or reproduce the work or
  any substantial part thereof in any material form whatever, to perform
  the work or any substantial part thereof in public or, if the work is
  unpublished, to publish the work or any substantial part thereof, and
  includes the sole right

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to produce, reproduce, perform or publish any
    translation of the work,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a dramatic work, to convert it into
    a novel or other non-dramatic work,

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a novel or other non-dramatic work,
    or of an artistic work, to convert it into a dramatic work, by way
    of performance in public or otherwise,

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a literary, dramatic or musical
    work, to make any sound recording, cinematograph film or other
    contrivance by means of which the work may be mechanically
    reproduced or performed,

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of any literary, dramatic, musical or
    artistic work, to reproduce, adapt and publicly present the work as
    a cinematographic work,

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} in the case of any literary, dramatic, musical or
    artistic work, to communicate the work to the public by
    telecommunication,

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} to present at a public exhibition, for a purpose
    other than sale or hire, an artistic work created after June 7,
    1988, other than a map, chart or plan,

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a computer program that can be
    reproduced in the ordinary course of its use, other than by a
    reproduction during its execution in conjunction with a machine,
    device or computer, to rent out the computer program,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a musical work, to rent out a sound
    recording in which the work is embodied, and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a work that is in the form of a
    tangible object, to sell or otherwise transfer ownership of the
    tangible object, as long as that ownership has never previously been
    transferred in or outside Canada with the authorization of the
    copyright owner,

  and to authorize any such acts.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Simultaneous fixing

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} A work that is communicated in the manner described
  in paragraph (1)(f) is fixed even if it is fixed simultaneously with
  its communication.

- [(1.2) to (4)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 1997, c. 24, s. 3\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-42, s. 3
- R.S., 1985, c. 10 (4th Supp.), s. 2
- 1988, c. 65, s. 62
- 1993, c. 23, s. 2, c. 44, s. 55
- 1997, c. 24, s. 3
- 2012, c. 20, s. 4
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-3-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 3"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 1997, c. 24,
s. 4\]]{.Repealed}

### [Works in which Copyright may Subsist]{.HTitleText2} {#h-102747 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions for subsistence of copyright

- **[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject
  to this Act, copyright shall subsist in Canada, for the term
  hereinafter mentioned, in every original literary, dramatic, musical
  and artistic work if any one of the following conditions is met:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of any work, whether published or
    unpublished, including a cinematographic work, the author was, at
    the date of the making of the work, a citizen or subject of, or a
    person ordinarily resident in, a treaty country;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a cinematographic work, whether
    published or unpublished, the maker, at the date of the making of
    the cinematographic work,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if a corporation, had its headquarters in a
      treaty country, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if a natural person, was a citizen or subject
      of, or a person ordinarily resident in, a treaty country; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a published work, including a
    cinematographic work,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in relation to subparagraph 2.2(1)(a)(i), the
      first publication in such a quantity as to satisfy the reasonable
      demands of the public, having regard to the nature of the work,
      occurred in a treaty country, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in relation to subparagraph 2.2(1)(a)(ii) or
      (iii), the first publication occurred in a treaty country.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Protection for older works

  [(1.01)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), a country that
  becomes a Berne Convention country, a WCT country or a WTO Member
  after the date of the making or publication of a work is deemed to
  have been a Berne Convention country, a WCT country or a WTO Member,
  as the case may be, at that date, subject to subsection (1.02) and
  sections 33 to 33.2.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(1.02)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1.01) does not confer copyright
  protection in Canada on a work whose term of copyright protection in
  the country referred to in that subsection had expired before that
  country became a Berne Convention country, a WCT country or a WTO
  Member, as the case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of subsections (1.01) and
  (1.02)

  [(1.03)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1.01) and (1.02) apply, and are
  deemed to have applied, regardless of whether the country in question
  became a Berne Convention country, a WCT country or a WTO Member
  before or after the coming into force of those subsections.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}First publication

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} The first publication described in subparagraph
  (1)(c)(i) or (ii) is deemed to have occurred in a treaty country
  notwithstanding that it in fact occurred previously elsewhere, if the
  interval between those two publications did not exceed thirty days.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} Copyright shall not subsist in Canada otherwise
  than as provided by subsection (1), except in so far as the protection
  conferred by this Act is extended as hereinafter provided to foreign
  countries to which this Act does not extend.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister may extend copyright to other
  countries

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the Minister certifies by notice, published in
  the [Canada Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/), that any country that
  is not a treaty country grants or has undertaken to grant, either by
  treaty, convention, agreement or law, to citizens of Canada, the
  benefit of copyright on substantially the same basis as to its own
  citizens or copyright protection substantially equal to that conferred
  by this Act, the country shall, for the purpose of the rights
  conferred by this Act, be treated as if it were a country to which
  this Act extends, and the Minister may give a certificate,
  notwithstanding that the remedies for enforcing the rights, or the
  restrictions on the importation of copies of works, under the law of
  such country, differ from those in this Act.

- [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 1994, c. 47, s. 57\]]{.Repealed}

- [(3) to (6)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 1997, c. 24, s. 5\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reciprocity protection preserved

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the protection to which a work
  is entitled by virtue of a notice published under subsection (2), or
  under that subsection as it read at any time before the coming into
  force of this subsection, is not affected by reason only of the
  country in question becoming a treaty country.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-42, s. 5
- 1993, c. 15, s. 2, c. 44, s. 57
- 1994, c. 47, s. 57
- 1997, c. 24, s. 5
- 2001, c. 34, s. 34
- 2012, c. 20, s. 5
:::
