:::

### [Ownership of Copyright]{.HTitleText2} {#h-103142 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ownership of copyright

**[[24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24 .sectionLabel}** The first owner of the
copyright

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in a performer's performance, is the performer;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in a sound recording, is the maker; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in a communication signal, is the broadcaster that
  broadcasts it.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-42, s. 24
- 1994, c. 47, s. 59
- 1997, c. 24, s. 14
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Assignment of rights

**[[25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25 .sectionLabel}** Subsections 13(4) to (7)
apply, with such modifications as the circumstances require, in respect
of the rights conferred by this Part on performers, makers of sound
recordings and broadcasters.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-42, s. 25
- 1993, c. 44, s. 62
- 1994, c. 47, s. 59
- 1997, c. 24, s. 14
:::

### [Performers' Rights --- WTO Countries]{.HTitleText2} {#h-103154 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Performer's performance in WTO country

- **[[26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-26 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  a performer's performance takes place on or after January 1, 1996 in a
  country that is a WTO Member, the performer has, as of the date of the
  performer's performance, a copyright in the performer's performance,
  consisting of the sole right to do the following in relation to the
  performer's performance or any substantial part thereof:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if it is not fixed, to communicate it to the public
    by telecommunication and to fix it in a sound recording, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if it has been fixed in a sound recording without
    the performer's authorization, to reproduce the fixation or any
    substantial part thereof,

  and to authorize any such acts.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where country joins WTO after Jan. 1,
  1996

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a performer's performance takes place on or
  after January 1, 1996 in a country that becomes a WTO Member after the
  date of the performer's performance, the performer has the copyright
  described in subsection (1) as of the date the country becomes a WTO
  Member.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Performer's performances before Jan. 1,
  1996

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where a performer's performance takes place before
  January 1, 1996 in a country that is a WTO Member, the performer has,
  as of January 1, 1996, the sole right to do and to authorize the act
  described in paragraph (1)(b).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where country joins WTO after Jan. 1,
  1996

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where a performer's performance takes place before
  January 1, 1996 in a country that becomes a WTO Member on or after
  January 1, 1996, the performer has the right described in
  subsection (3) as of the date the country becomes a WTO Member.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Term of performer's rights

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The rights conferred by this section subsist for the
  remainder of the calendar year in which the performer's performance
  takes place and a period of fifty years following the end of that
  calendar year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Assignment of rights

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Subsections 13(4) to (7) apply, with such
  modifications as the circumstances require, in respect of a
  performer's rights conferred by this section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding an assignment of a performer's right
  conferred by this section, the performer, as well as the assignee, may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} prevent the reproduction of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any fixation of the performer's performance, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any substantial part of such a fixation,

    where the fixation was made without the performer's consent or the
    assignee's consent; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prevent the importation of any fixation of the
    performer's performance, or any reproduction of such a fixation,
    that the importer knows or ought to have known was made without the
    performer's consent or the assignee's consent.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-42, s. 26
- R.S., 1985, c. 10 (4th Supp.), s. 17(F)
- 1993, c. 44, s. 63
- 1994, c. 47, s. 59
- 1997, c. 24, s. 14
:::

## [PART III]{.HLabel1}[Infringement of Copyright and Moral Rights and Exceptions to Infringement]{.HTitleText1} {#h-103180 .Part}

### [Infringement of Copyright]{.HTitleText2} {#h-103181 .Subheading}

#### [General]{.HTitleText3} {#h-103182 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Infringement generally

- **[[27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-27 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} It is
  an infringement of copyright for any person to do, without the consent
  of the owner of the copyright, anything that by this Act only the
  owner of the copyright has the right to do.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Secondary infringement

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} It is an infringement of copyright for any person to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} sell or rent out,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} distribute to such an extent as to affect
    prejudicially the owner of the copyright,

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} by way of trade distribute, expose or offer for
    sale or rental, or exhibit in public,

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} possess for the purpose of doing anything referred
    to in paragraphs (a) to (c), or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} import into Canada for the purpose of doing
    anything referred to in paragraphs (a) to (c),

  a copy of a work, sound recording or fixation of a performer's
  performance or of a communication signal that the person knows or
  should have known infringes copyright or would infringe copyright if
  it had been made in Canada by the person who made it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Clarification

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, a copy made outside Canada
  does not infringe copyright under subsection (2) if, had it been made
  in Canada, it would have been made under a limitation or exception
  under this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Secondary infringement  --- exportation

  [(2.11)]{.lawlabel} It is an infringement of copyright for any person,
  for the purpose of doing anything referred to in paragraphs (2)(a) to
  (c), to export or attempt to export a copy  ---  of a work, sound
  recording or fixation of a performer's performance or of a
  communication signal  ---  that the person knows or should have known
  was made without the consent of the owner of the copyright in the
  country where the copy was made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2.12)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (2.11) does not apply with respect to a
  copy that was made under a limitation or exception under this Act or,
  if it was made outside Canada, that would have been made under such a
  limitation or exception had it been made in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Secondary infringement related to
  lesson

  [(2.2)]{.lawlabel} It is an infringement of copyright for any person
  to do any of the following acts with respect to anything that the
  person knows or should have known is a lesson, as defined in
  subsection 30.01(1), or a fixation of one:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to sell it or to rent it out;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to distribute it to an extent that the owner of the
    copyright in the work or other subject-matter that is included in
    the lesson is prejudicially affected;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} by way of trade, to distribute it, expose or offer
    it for sale or rental or exhibit it in public;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} to possess it for the purpose of doing anything
    referred to in any of paragraphs (a) to (c);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} to communicate it by telecommunication to any
    person other than a person referred to in paragraph 30.01(3)(a); or

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} to circumvent or contravene any measure taken in
    conformity with paragraph 30.01(6)(b), (c) or (d).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Infringement --- provision of services

  [(2.3)]{.lawlabel} It is an infringement of copyright for a person, by
  means of the Internet or another digital network, to provide a service
  primarily for the purpose of enabling acts of copyright infringement
  if an actual infringement of copyright occurs by means of the Internet
  or another digital network as a result of the use of that service.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Factors

  [(2.4)]{.lawlabel} In determining whether a person has infringed
  copyright under subsection (2.3), the court may consider

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} whether the person expressly or implicitly marketed
    or promoted the service as one that could be used to enable acts of
    copyright infringement;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} whether the person had knowledge that the service
    was used to enable a significant number of acts of copyright
    infringement;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} whether the service has significant uses other than
    to enable acts of copyright infringement;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the person's ability, as part of providing the
    service, to limit acts of copyright infringement, and any action
    taken by the person to do so;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any benefits the person received as a result of
    enabling the acts of copyright infringement; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the economic viability of the provision of the
    service if it were not used to enable acts of copyright
    infringement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Knowledge of importer

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In determining whether there is an infringement under
  subsection (2) in the case of an activity referred to in any of
  paragraphs (2)(a) to (d) in relation to a copy that was imported in
  the circumstances referred to in paragraph (2)(e), it is irrelevant
  whether the importer knew or should have known that the importation of
  the copy infringed copyright.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Plates

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} It is an infringement of copyright for any person to
  make or possess a plate that has been specifically designed or adapted
  for the purpose of making infringing copies of a work or other
  subject-matter.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Public performance for profit

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} It is an infringement of copyright for any person,
  for profit, to permit a theatre or other place of entertainment to be
  used for the performance in public of a work or other subject-matter
  without the consent of the owner of the copyright unless that person
  was not aware, and had no reasonable ground for suspecting, that the
  performance would be an infringement of copyright.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-42, s. 27
- R.S., 1985, c. 1 (3rd Supp.), s. 13, c. 10 (4th Supp.), s. 5
- 1993, c. 44, s. 64
- 1997, c. 24, s. 15
- 2012, c. 20, s. 18
- 2014, c. 32, s. 3
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-27-20121107.html "Link to previous version of section 27"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Parallel Importation of Books]{.HTitleText3} {#h-103226 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Importation of books

- **[[27.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-27.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to any regulations made
  under subsection (6), it is an infringement of copyright in a book for
  any person to import the book where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} copies of the book were made with the consent of
    the owner of the copyright in the book in the country where the
    copies were made, but were imported without the consent of the owner
    of the copyright in the book in Canada; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person knows or should have known that the book
    would infringe copyright if it was made in Canada by the importer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Secondary infringement

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to any regulations made under subsection (6),
  where the circumstances described in paragraph (1)(a) exist, it is an
  infringement of copyright in an imported book for any person who knew
  or should have known that the book would infringe copyright if it was
  made in Canada by the importer to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} sell or rent out the book;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} by way of trade, distribute, expose or offer for
    sale or rental, or exhibit in public, the book; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} possess the book for the purpose of any of the
    activities referred to in paragraph (a) or (b).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) and (2) only apply where there is an
  exclusive distributor of the book and the acts described in those
  subsections take place in the part of Canada or in respect of the
  particular sector of the market for which the person is the exclusive
  distributor.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exclusive distributor

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An exclusive distributor is deemed, for the purposes
  of entitlement to any of the remedies under Part IV in relation to an
  infringement under this section, to derive an interest in the
  copyright in question by licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} No exclusive distributor, copyright owner or
  exclusive licensee is entitled to a remedy under Part IV in relation
  to an infringement under this section unless, before the infringement
  occurred, notice has been given within the prescribed time and in the
  prescribed manner to the person referred to in subsection (1) or (2),
  as the case may be, that there is an exclusive distributor of the
  book.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may, by regulation, establish
  terms and conditions for the importation of certain categories of
  books, including remaindered books, books intended solely for
  re-export and books imported by special order.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 15
:::

**[[28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 1997, c. 24,
s. 15\]]{.Repealed}

**[[28.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28.01 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 1997,
c. 24, s. 16\]]{.Repealed}

**[[28.02 and 28.03]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28.02_and_28.03
.sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 1997, c. 24, s. 17\]]{.Repealed}

### [Moral Rights Infringement]{.HTitleText2} {#h-103250 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Infringement generally

**[[28.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28.1 .sectionLabel}** Any act or omission
that is contrary to any of the moral rights of the author of a work or
of the performer of a performer's performance is, in the absence of the
author's or performer's consent, an infringement of those rights.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 10 (4th Supp.), s. 6
- 2012, c. 20, s. 19
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-28.1-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 28.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Nature of right of integrity

- **[[28.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The author's or performer's right to
  the integrity of a work or performer's performance is infringed only
  if the work or the performance is, to the prejudice of its author's or
  performer's honour or reputation,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} distorted, mutilated or otherwise modified; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} used in association with a product, service, cause
    or institution.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where prejudice deemed

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a painting, sculpture or engraving,
  the prejudice referred to in subsection (1) shall be deemed to have
  occurred as a result of any distortion, mutilation or other
  modification of the work.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When work not distorted, etc.

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a change in the location of a work, the physical
    means by which a work is exposed or the physical structure
    containing a work, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} steps taken in good faith to restore or preserve
    the work

  shall not, by that act alone, constitute a distortion, mutilation or
  other modification of the work.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 10 (4th Supp.), s. 6
- 2012, c. 20, s. 20
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-28.2-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 28.2"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Exceptions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-103269 .Subheading}

#### [Fair Dealing]{.HTitleText3} {#h-103270 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Research, private study, etc.

**[[29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29 .sectionLabel}** Fair dealing for the
purpose of research, private study, education, parody or satire does not
infringe copyright.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-42, s. 29
- R.S., 1985, c. 10 (4th Supp.), s. 7
- 1994, c. 47, s. 61
- 1997, c. 24, s. 18
- 2012, c. 20, s. 21
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-29-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 29"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Criticism or review

**[[29.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29.1 .sectionLabel}** Fair dealing for the
purpose of criticism or review does not infringe copyright if the
following are mentioned:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the source; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if given in the source, the name of the

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} author, in the case of a work,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} performer, in the case of a performer's
    performance,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} maker, in the case of a sound recording, or

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} broadcaster, in the case of a communication
    signal.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 18
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}News reporting

**[[29.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29.2 .sectionLabel}** Fair dealing for the
purpose of news reporting does not infringe copyright if the following
are mentioned:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the source; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if given in the source, the name of the

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} author, in the case of a work,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} performer, in the case of a performer's
    performance,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} maker, in the case of a sound recording, or

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} broadcaster, in the case of a communication
    signal.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 18
:::

#### [Non-commercial User-generated Content]{.HTitleText3} {#h-103295 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-commercial user-generated content

- **[[29.21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29.21
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} It is not an infringement of
  copyright for an individual to use an existing work or other
  subject-matter or copy of one, which has been published or otherwise
  made available to the public, in the creation of a new work or other
  subject-matter in which copyright subsists and for the individual ---
  or, with the individual's authorization, a member of their household
  --- to use the new work or other subject-matter or to authorize an
  intermediary to disseminate it, if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the use of, or the authorization to disseminate,
    the new work or other subject-matter is done solely for
    non-commercial purposes;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the source --- and, if given in the source, the
    name of the author, performer, maker or broadcaster --- of the
    existing work or other subject-matter or copy of it are mentioned,
    if it is reasonable in the circumstances to do so;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the individual had reasonable grounds to believe
    that the existing work or other subject-matter or copy of it, as the
    case may be, was not infringing copyright; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the use of, or the authorization to disseminate,
    the new work or other subject-matter does not have a substantial
    adverse effect, financial or otherwise, on the exploitation or
    potential exploitation of the existing work or other subject-matter
    --- or copy of it --- or on an existing or potential market for it,
    including that the new work or other subject-matter is not a
    substitute for the existing one.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The following definitions apply in subsection (1).

  [[intermediary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[intermediary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person or entity who
      regularly provides space or means for works or other
      subject-matter to be enjoyed by the
      public. ([intermédiaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[use]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[use]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means to do anything that by this Act
      the owner of the copyright has the sole right to do, other than
      the right to authorize anything. ([utiliser]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- 2012, c. 20, s. 22
:::

#### [Reproduction for Private Purposes]{.HTitleText3} {#h-103309 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reproduction for private purposes

- **[[29.22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29.22
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} It is not an infringement of
  copyright for an individual to reproduce a work or other
  subject-matter or any substantial part of a work or other
  subject-matter if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the copy of the work or other subject-matter from
    which the reproduction is made is not an infringing copy;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the individual legally obtained the copy of the
    work or other subject-matter from which the reproduction is made,
    other than by borrowing it or renting it, and owns or is authorized
    to use the medium or device on which it is reproduced;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the individual, in order to make the reproduction,
    did not circumvent, as defined in section 41, a technological
    protection measure, as defined in that section, or cause one to be
    circumvented;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the individual does not give the reproduction away;
    and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the reproduction is used only for the individual's
    private purposes.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Meaning of [[medium or
  device]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (1)(b), a [[medium or
  device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes digital memory in which a work
  or subject-matter may be stored for the purpose of allowing the
  telecommunication of the work or other subject-matter through the
  Internet or other digital network.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation --- audio recording medium

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a work or other subject-matter that is
  a musical work embodied in a sound recording, a performer's
  performance of a musical work embodied in a sound recording or a sound
  recording in which a musical work or a performer's performance of a
  musical work is embodied, subsection (1) does not apply if the
  reproduction is made onto an audio recording medium as defined in
  section 79.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation --- destruction of
  reproductions

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply if the individual gives
  away, rents or sells the copy of the work or other subject-matter from
  which the reproduction is made without first destroying all
  reproductions of that copy that the individual has made under that
  subsection.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2012, c. 20, s. 22
:::

#### [Fixing Signals and Recording Programs for Later Listening or Viewing]{.HTitleText3} {#h-103326 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reproduction for later listening or
viewing

- **[[29.23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29.23
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} It is not an infringement of
  copyright for an individual to fix a communication signal, to
  reproduce a work or sound recording that is being broadcast or to fix
  or reproduce a performer's performance that is being broadcast, in
  order to record a program for the purpose of listening to or viewing
  it later, if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the individual receives the program legally;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the individual, in order to record the program, did
    not circumvent, as defined in section 41, a technological protection
    measure, as defined in that section, or cause one to be
    circumvented;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the individual makes no more than one recording of
    the program;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the individual keeps the recording no longer than
    is reasonably necessary in order to listen to or view the program at
    a more convenient time;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the individual does not give the recording away;
    and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the recording is used only for the individual's
    private purposes.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply if the individual
  receives the work, performer's performance or sound recording under an
  on-demand service.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The following definitions apply in this section.

  [[broadcast]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[broadcast]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any transmission of a work
      or other subject-matter by telecommunication for reception by the
      public, but does not include a transmission that is made solely
      for performance in public. ([radiodiffusion]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[on-demand service]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[on-demand service]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a service that
      allows a person to receive works, performer's performances and
      sound recordings at times of their choosing. ([service sur
      demande]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- 2012, c. 20, s. 22
:::

#### [Backup Copies]{.HTitleText3} {#h-103344 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Backup copies

- **[[29.24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29.24
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} It is not an infringement of
  copyright in a work or other subject-matter for a person who owns ---
  or has a licence to use --- a copy of the work or subject-matter (in
  this section referred to as the "source copy") to reproduce the source
  copy if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person does so solely for backup purposes in
    case the source copy is lost, damaged or otherwise rendered
    unusable;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the source copy is not an infringing copy;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the person, in order to make the reproduction, did
    not circumvent, as defined in section 41, a technological protection
    measure, as defined in that section, or cause one to be
    circumvented; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the person does not give any of the reproductions
    away.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Backup copy becomes source copy

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the source copy is lost, damaged or otherwise
  rendered unusable, one of the reproductions made under subsection (1)
  becomes the source copy.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Destruction

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The person shall immediately destroy all
  reproductions made under subsection (1) after the person ceases to
  own, or to have a licence to use, the source copy.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2012, c. 20, s. 22
:::

#### [Acts Undertaken without Motive of Gain]{.HTitleText3} {#h-103358 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Motive of gain

- **[[29.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29.3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  action referred to in section 29.4, 29.5, 30.2 or 30.21 may be carried
  out with motive of gain.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cost recovery

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An educational institution, library, archive or
  museum, or person acting under its authority does not have a motive of
  gain where it or the person acting under its authority, does anything
  referred to in section 29.4, 29.5, 30.2 or 30.21 and recovers no more
  than the costs, including overhead costs, associated with doing that
  act.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 18
:::

#### [Educational Institutions]{.HTitleText3} {#h-103366 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reproduction for instruction

- **[[29.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29.4 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} It
  is not an infringement of copyright for an educational institution or
  a person acting under its authority for the purposes of education or
  training on its premises to reproduce a work, or do any other
  necessary act, in order to display it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reproduction for examinations, etc.

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} It is not an infringement of copyright for an
  educational institution or a person acting under its authority to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} reproduce, translate or perform in public on the
    premises of the educational institution, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} communicate by telecommunication to the public
    situated on the premises of the educational institution

  a work or other subject-matter as required for a test or examination.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}If work commercially available

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Except in the case of manual reproduction, the
  exemption from copyright infringement provided by subsections (1)
  and (2) does not apply if the work or other subject-matter is
  commercially available, within the meaning of paragraph (a) of the
  definition [[commercially available]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section
  2, in a medium that is appropriate for the purposes referred to in
  those subsections.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 18
- 2012, c. 20, s. 23
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-29.4-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 29.4"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Performances

**[[29.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29.5 .sectionLabel}** It is not an
infringement of copyright for an educational institution or a person
acting under its authority to do the following acts if they are done on
the premises of an educational institution for educational or training
purposes and not for profit, before an audience consisting primarily of
students of the educational institution, instructors acting under the
authority of the educational institution or any person who is directly
responsible for setting a curriculum for the educational institution:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the live performance in public, primarily by students
  of the educational institution, of a work;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the performance in public of a sound recording, or of
  a work or performer's performance that is embodied in a sound
  recording, as long as the sound recording is not an infringing copy or
  the person responsible for the performance has no reasonable grounds
  to believe that it is an infringing copy;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the performance in public of a work or other
  subject-matter at the time of its communication to the public by
  telecommunication; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the performance in public of a cinematographic work,
  as long as the work is not an infringing copy or the person
  responsible for the performance has no reasonable grounds to believe
  that it is an infringing copy.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 18
- 2012, c. 20, s. 24
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-29.5-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 29.5"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}News and commentary

- **[[29.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29.6 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} It
  is not an infringement of copyright for an educational institution or
  a person acting under its authority to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} make, at the time of its communication to the
    public by telecommunication, a single copy of a news program or a
    news commentary program, excluding documentaries, for the purposes
    of performing the copy for the students of the educational
    institution for educational or training purposes; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} perform the copy in public before an audience
    consisting primarily of students of the educational institution on
    its premises for educational or training purposes.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2012, c. 20, s. 25\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 18
- 2012, c. 20, s. 25
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-29.6-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 29.6"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reproduction of broadcast

- **[[29.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29.7
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2) and
  section 29.9, it is not an infringement of copyright for an
  educational institution or a person acting under its authority to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} make a single copy of a work or other
    subject-matter at the time that it is communicated to the public by
    telecommunication; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} keep the copy for up to thirty days to decide
    whether to perform the copy for educational or training purposes.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Royalties for reproduction

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An educational institution that has not destroyed the
  copy by the expiration of the thirty days infringes copyright in the
  work or other subject-matter unless it pays any royalties, and
  complies with any terms and conditions, fixed under this Act for the
  making of the copy.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Royalties for performance

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} It is not an infringement of copyright for the
  educational institution or a person acting under its authority to
  perform the copy in public for educational or training purposes on the
  premises of the educational institution before an audience consisting
  primarily of students of the educational institution if the
  educational institution pays the royalties and complies with any terms
  and conditions fixed under this Act for the performance in public.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 18
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unlawful reception

**[[29.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29.8 .sectionLabel}** The exceptions to
infringement of copyright provided for under sections 29.5 to 29.7 do
not apply where the communication to the public by telecommunication was
received by unlawful means.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 18
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Records and marking

- **[[29.9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29.9
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where an educational institution or
  person acting under its authority

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2012, c. 20, s. 26\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} makes a copy of a work or other subject-matter
    communicated to the public by telecommunication and performs it
    pursuant to section 29.7,

  the educational institution shall keep a record of the information
  prescribed by regulation in relation to the making of the copy, the
  destruction of it or any performance in public of it for which
  royalties are payable under this Act and shall, in addition, mark the
  copy in the manner prescribed by regulation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Board may, with the approval of the Governor in
  Council, make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the information in relation to the
    making, destruction, performance and marking of copies that must be
    kept under subsection (1),

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the manner and form in which records
    referred to in that subsection must be kept and copies destroyed or
    marked, and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} respecting the sending of information to collective
    societies that carry on the business of collecting royalties
    referred to in subsection 29.7(2) or (3).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 18
- 2012, c. 20, s. 26
- [2018, c. 27, s. 282](#1039398-1121272){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039398-1121272.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-29.9-20121107.html "Link to previous version of section 29.9"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Literary collections

**[[30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30 .sectionLabel}** The publication in a
collection, mainly composed of non-copyright matter, intended for the
use of educational institutions, and so described in the title and in
any advertisements issued by the publisher, of short passages from
published literary works in which copyright subsists and not themselves
published for the use of educational institutions, does not infringe
copyright in those published literary works if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} not more than two passages from works by the same
  author are published by the same publisher within five years;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the source from which the passages are taken is
  acknowledged; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name of the author, if given in the source, is
  mentioned.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-42, s. 30
- R.S., 1985, c. 10 (4th Supp.), s. 7
- 1997, c. 24, s. 18
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Meaning of
[[lesson]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[30.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30.01
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section,
  [[lesson]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a lesson, test or examination, or
  part of one, in which, or during the course of which, an act is done
  in respect of a work or other subject-matter by an educational
  institution or a person acting under its authority that would
  otherwise be an infringement of copyright but is permitted under a
  limitation or exception under this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} This section does not apply so as to permit any act
  referred to in paragraph (3)(a), (b) or (c) with respect to a work or
  other subject-matter whose use in the lesson constitutes an
  infringement of copyright or for whose use in the lesson the consent
  of the copyright owner is required.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Communication by telecommunication

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (6), it is not an infringement
  of copyright for an educational institution or a person acting under
  its authority

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to communicate a lesson to the public by
    telecommunication for educational or training purposes, if that
    public consists only of students who are enrolled in a course of
    which the lesson forms a part or of other persons acting under the
    authority of the educational institution;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to make a fixation of the lesson for the purpose of
    the act referred to in paragraph (a); or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to do any other act that is necessary for the
    purpose of the acts referred to in paragraphs (a) and (b).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Participation by telecommunication

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A student who is enrolled in a course of which the
  lesson forms a part is deemed to be a person on the premises of the
  educational institution when the student participates in or receives
  the lesson by means of communication by telecommunication under
  paragraph (3)(a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reproducing lessons

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} It is not an infringement of copyright for a student
  who has received a lesson by means of communication by
  telecommunication under paragraph (3)(a) to reproduce the lesson in
  order to be able to listen to or view it at a more convenient time.
  However, the student shall destroy the reproduction within 30 days
  after the day on which the students who are enrolled in the course to
  which the lesson relates have received their final course evaluations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The educational institution and any person acting
  under its authority, except a student, shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} destroy any fixation of the lesson within 30 days
    after the day on which the students who are enrolled in the course
    to which the lesson relates have received their final course
    evaluations;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} take measures that can reasonably be expected to
    limit the communication by telecommunication of the lesson to the
    persons referred to in paragraph (3)(a);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} take, in relation to the communication by
    telecommunication of the lesson in digital form, measures that can
    reasonably be expected to prevent the students from fixing,
    reproducing or communicating the lesson other than as they may do
    under this section; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} take, in relation to a communication by
    telecommunication in digital form, any measure prescribed by
    regulation.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2012, c. 20, s. 27
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- digital reproduction of
works

- **[[30.02]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30.02
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (3) to (5),
  it is not an infringement of copyright for an educational institution
  that has a reprographic reproduction licence under which the
  institution is authorized to make reprographic reproductions of works
  in a collective society's repertoire for an educational or training
  purpose

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to make a digital reproduction --- of the same
    general nature and extent as the reprographic reproduction
    authorized under the licence --- of a paper form of any of those
    works;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to communicate the digital reproduction by
    telecommunication for an educational or training purpose to persons
    acting under the authority of the institution; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to do any other act that is necessary for the
    purpose of the acts referred to in paragraphs (a) and (b).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (3) to (5), it is not an
  infringement of copyright for a person acting under the authority of
  the educational institution to whom the work has been communicated
  under paragraph (1)(b) to print one copy of the work.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An educational institution that makes a digital
  reproduction of a work under paragraph (1)(a) shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} pay to the collective society, with respect to all
    the persons to whom the digital reproduction is communicated by the
    institution under paragraph (1)(b), the royalties that would be
    payable if one reprographic reproduction were distributed by the
    institution to each of those persons, and comply with the licence
    terms and conditions applicable to a reprographic reproduction to
    the extent that they are reasonably applicable to a digital
    reproduction;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} take measures to prevent the digital reproduction
    from being communicated by telecommunication to any persons who are
    not acting under the authority of the institution;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} take measures to prevent a person to whom the work
    has been communicated under paragraph (1)(b) from printing more than
    one copy, and to prevent any other reproduction or communication of
    the digital reproduction; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} take any measure prescribed by regulation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An educational institution may not make a digital
  reproduction of a work under paragraph (1)(a) if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the institution has entered into a digital
    reproduction agreement respecting the work with a collective society
    under which the institution may make a digital reproduction of the
    work, may communicate the digital reproduction by telecommunication
    to persons acting under the authority of the institution and may
    permit those persons to print at least one copy of the work;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} there is a tariff approved under section 70 that is
    applicable to the digital reproduction of the work, to the
    communication of the digital reproduction by telecommunication to
    persons acting under the authority of the institution and to the
    printing by those persons of at least one copy of the work; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the institution has been informed by the collective
    society that is authorized to enter into reprographic agreements
    with respect to the work that the owner of the copyright in the work
    has informed it, under subsection (5), that the owner refuses to
    authorize the collective society to enter into a digital
    reproduction agreement with respect to the work.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If the owner of the copyright in a work informs the
  collective society that is authorized to enter into reprographic
  agreements with respect to the work that the owner refuses to
  authorize it to enter into digital reproduction agreements with
  respect to the work, the collective society shall inform the
  educational institutions with which it has entered into reprographic
  reproduction agreements with respect to the work that they are not
  permitted to make digital reproductions under subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deeming provision

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The owner of the copyright in a work who, in respect
  of the work, has authorized a collective society to enter into a
  reprographic reproduction agreement with an educational institution is
  deemed to have authorized the society to enter into a digital
  reproduction agreement with the institution --- subject to the same
  restrictions as a reprographic reproduction agreement --- unless the
  owner has refused to give this authorization under subsection (5) or
  has authorized another collective society to enter into a digital
  reproduction agreement with respect to the work.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum amount that may be recovered

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} In proceedings against an educational institution for
  making a digital reproduction of a paper form of a work, or for
  communicating such a reproduction by telecommunication for an
  educational or training purpose to persons acting under the authority
  of the institution, the owner of the copyright in the work may not
  recover an amount more than

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case where there is a digital reproduction
    licence that meets the conditions described in paragraph (4)(a) in
    respect of the work --- or, if none exists in respect of the work,
    in respect of a work of the same category --- the amount of
    royalties that would be payable under that licence in respect of
    those acts or, if there is more than one applicable licence, the
    greatest amount of royalties payable under any of those licences;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case where there is no licence described in
    paragraph (a) but there is a reprographic reproduction licence in
    respect of the work --- or, if none exists in respect of the work,
    in respect of a work of the same category --- the amount of
    royalties that would be payable under that licence in respect of
    those acts or, if there is more than one applicable licence, the
    greatest amount of royalties payable under any of those licences.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No damages

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} The owner of the copyright in a work may not recover
  any damages against a person acting under the authority of the
  educational institution who, in respect of a digital reproduction of
  the work that is communicated to the person by telecommunication,
  prints one copy of the work if, at the time of the printing, it was
  reasonable for the person to believe that the communication was made
  in accordance with paragraph (1)(b).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Royalties --- digital reproduction
agreement

- **[[30.03]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30.03
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If an educational institution has
  paid royalties to a collective society for the digital reproduction of
  a work under paragraph 30.02(3)(a) and afterwards the institution
  enters into a digital reproduction agreement described in paragraph
  30.02(4)(a) with any collective society,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the institution would --- under
    that digital reproduction agreement --- pay a greater amount of
    royalties for the digital reproduction of that work than what was
    payable under paragraph 30.02(3)(a), the institution shall pay to
    the collective society to which it paid royalties under that
    paragraph the difference between

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the amount of royalties that the institution
      would have had to pay for the digital reproduction of that work if
      the agreement had been entered into on the day on which the
      institution first made a digital reproduction under paragraph
      30.02(1)(a), and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the amount of royalties that the institution
      paid to the society under paragraph 30.02(3)(a) for the digital
      reproduction of that work from the day on which that paragraph
      comes into force until the day on which they enter into the
      digital reproduction agreement; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the institution would --- under
    that digital reproduction agreement --- pay a lesser amount of
    royalties for the digital reproduction of that work than what was
    payable under paragraph 30.02(3)(a), the collective society to which
    the institution paid royalties under that paragraph shall pay to the
    institution the difference between

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the amount of royalties that the institution paid
      to the society under paragraph 30.02(3)(a) for the digital
      reproduction of that work from the day on which that paragraph
      comes into force until the day on which they enter into the
      digital reproduction agreement, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the amount of royalties that the institution
      would have had to pay for the digital reproduction of that work if
      the agreement had been entered into on the day on which the
      institution first made a digital reproduction under paragraph
      30.02(1)(a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Royalties --- tariff

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If an educational institution has paid royalties to a
  collective society for the digital reproduction of a work under
  paragraph 30.02(3)(a) and afterwards a tariff applies to the digital
  reproduction of that work under paragraph 30.02(4)(b),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the institution would --- under
    the tariff --- pay a greater amount of royalties for the digital
    reproduction of that work than what was payable under paragraph
    30.02(3)(a), the institution shall pay to the collective society to
    which it paid royalties under that paragraph the difference between

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the amount of royalties that the institution
      would have had to pay for the digital reproduction of that work if
      the tariff had been approved on the day on which the institution
      first made a digital reproduction under paragraph 30.02(1)(a), and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the amount of royalties that the institution
      paid to the society under paragraph 30.02(3)(a) for the digital
      reproduction of that work from the day on which that paragraph
      comes into force until the day on which the tariff is approved;
      and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the institution would --- under
    the tariff --- pay a lesser amount of royalties for the digital
    reproduction of that work than what was payable under paragraph
    30.02(3)(a), the collective society to which the institution paid
    royalties under that paragraph shall pay to the institution the
    difference between

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the amount of royalties that the institution paid
      to the society under paragraph 30.02(3)(a) for the digital
      reproduction of that work from the day on which that paragraph
      comes into force until the day on which the tariff is approved,
      and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the amount of royalties that the institution
      would have had to pay for the digital reproduction of that work if
      the tariff had been approved on the day on which the institution
      first made a digital reproduction under paragraph 30.02(1)(a).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Work available through Internet

- **[[30.04]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30.04
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) to (5),
  it is not an infringement of copyright for an educational institution,
  or a person acting under the authority of one, to do any of the
  following acts for educational or training purposes in respect of a
  work or other subject-matter that is available through the Internet:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} reproduce it;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} communicate it to the public by telecommunication,
    if that public primarily consists of students of the educational
    institution or other persons acting under its authority;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} perform it in public, if that public primarily
    consists of students of the educational institution or other persons
    acting under its authority; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} do any other act that is necessary for the purpose
    of the acts referred to in paragraphs (a) to (c).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply unless the educational
  institution or person acting under its authority, in doing any of the
  acts described in that subsection in respect of the work or other
  subject-matter, mentions the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the source; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if given in the source, the name of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the author, in the case of a work,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the performer, in the case of a performer's
      performance,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the maker, in the case of a sound recording,
      and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the broadcaster, in the case of a communication
      signal.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply if the work or other
  subject-matter --- or the Internet site where it is posted --- is
  protected by a technological protection measure that restricts access
  to the work or other subject-matter or to the Internet site.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not permit a person to do any act
  described in that subsection in respect of a work or other
  subject-matter if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that work or other subject-matter --- or the
    Internet site where it is posted --- is protected by a technological
    protection measure that restricts the doing of that act; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a clearly visible notice --- and not merely the
    copyright symbol --- prohibiting that act is posted at the Internet
    site where the work or other subject-matter is posted or on the work
    or other subject-matter itself.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply if the educational
  institution or person acting under its authority knows or should have
  known that the work or other subject-matter was made available through
  the Internet without the consent of the copyright owner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations for the
  purposes of paragraph (4)(b) prescribing what constitutes a clearly
  visible notice.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2012, c. 20, s. 27
:::

#### [Libraries, Archives and Museums]{.HTitleText3} {#h-103529 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Management and maintenance of collection

- **[[30.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} It
  is not an infringement of copyright for a library, archive or museum
  or a person acting under the authority of a library, archive or museum
  to make, for the maintenance or management of its permanent collection
  or the permanent collection of another library, archive or museum, a
  copy of a work or other subject-matter, whether published or
  unpublished, in its permanent collection

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the original is rare or unpublished and is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} deteriorating, damaged or lost, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} at risk of deterioration or becoming damaged or
      lost;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of on-site consultation if the
    original cannot be viewed, handled or listened to because of its
    condition or because of the atmospheric conditions in which it must
    be kept;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in an alternative format if the library, archive or
    museum or a person acting under the authority of the library,
    archive or museum considers that the original is currently in a
    format that is obsolete or is becoming obsolete, or that the
    technology required to use the original is unavailable or is
    becoming unavailable;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of internal record-keeping and
    cataloguing;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} for insurance purposes or police investigations; or

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if necessary for restoration.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraphs (1)(a) to (c) do not apply where an
  appropriate copy is commercially available in a medium and of a
  quality that is appropriate for the purposes of subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Destruction of intermediate copies

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a person must make an intermediate copy in order
  to make a copy under subsection (1), the person must destroy the
  intermediate copy as soon as it is no longer needed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations with
  respect to the procedure for making copies under subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 18
- 1999, c. 31, s. 59(E)
- 2012, c. 20, s. 28
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-30.1-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 30.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Research or private study

- **[[30.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30.2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} It
  is not an infringement of copyright for a library, archive or museum
  or a person acting under its authority to do anything on behalf of any
  person that the person may do personally under section 29 or 29.1.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copies of articles for research, etc.

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} It is not an infringement of copyright for a library,
  archive or museum or a person acting under the authority of a library,
  archive or museum to make, by reprographic reproduction, for any
  person requesting to use the copy for research or private study, a
  copy of a work that is, or that is contained in, an article published
  in

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a scholarly, scientific or technical periodical; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a newspaper or periodical, other than a scholarly,
    scientific or technical periodical, if the newspaper or periodical
    was published more than one year before the copy is made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (2)(b) does not apply in respect of a work
  of fiction or poetry or a dramatic or musical work.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A library, archive or museum may provide the person
  for whom the copy is made under subsection (2) with the copy only on
  the condition that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person is provided with a single copy of the
    work; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the library, archive or museum informs the person
    that the copy is to be used solely for research or private study and
    that any use of the copy for a purpose other than research or
    private study may require the authorization of the copyright owner
    of the work in question.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Patrons of other libraries, etc.

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (5.02), a library, archive or
  museum, or a person acting under the authority of one, may do, on
  behalf of a patron of another library, archive or museum, anything
  under subsection (1) or (2) that it is authorized by this section to
  do on behalf of one of its own patrons.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deeming

  [(5.01)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of subsection (5), the making of a
  copy of a work other than by reprographic reproduction is deemed to be
  a making of a copy of the work that may be done under subsection (2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation regarding copies in digital
  form

  [(5.02)]{.lawlabel} A library, archive or museum, or a person acting
  under the authority of one, may, under subsection (5), provide a copy
  in digital form to a person who has requested it through another
  library, archive or museum if the providing library, archive or museum
  or person takes measures to prevent the person who has requested it
  from

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} making any reproduction of the digital copy,
    including any paper copies, other than printing one copy of it;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} communicating the digital copy to any other person;
    and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} using the digital copy for more than five business
    days from the day on which the person first uses it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Destruction of intermediate copies

  [(5.1)]{.lawlabel} Where an intermediate copy is made in order to copy
  a work referred to in subsection (5), once the copy is given to the
  patron, the intermediate copy must be destroyed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may, for the purposes of this
  section, make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} defining "newspaper" and "periodical";

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} defining scholarly, scientific and technical
    periodicals;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the information to be recorded about
    any action taken under subsection (1) or (5) and the manner and form
    in which the information is to be kept; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the manner and form in which the
    conditions set out in subsection (4) are to be met.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 18
- 2012, c. 20, s. 29
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-30.2-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 30.2"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copying works deposited in archive

- **[[30.21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30.21
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (3) and
  (3.1), it is not an infringement of copyright for an archive to make,
  for any person requesting to use the copy for research or private
  study, a copy of an unpublished work that is deposited in the archive
  and provide the person with it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} When a person deposits a work in an archive, the
  archive must give the person notice that it may copy the work in
  accordance with this section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions for copying of works

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The archive may copy the work only on the condition
  that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person who deposited the work, if a copyright
    owner, did not, at the time the work was deposited, prohibit its
    copying; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} copying has not been prohibited by any other owner
    of copyright in the work.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Condition for providing copy

  [(3.1)]{.lawlabel} The archive may provide the person for whom a copy
  is made under subsection (1) with the copy only on the condition that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person is provided with a single copy of the
    work; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the archive informs the person that the copy is to
    be used solely for research or private study and that any use of the
    copy for a purpose other than research or private study may require
    the authorization of the copyright owner of the work in question.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may prescribe by regulation
  the manner and form in which the conditions set out in subsections (3)
  and (3.1) may be met.

- [(5) to (7)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2004, c. 11, s. 21\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 18
- 1999, c. 31, s. 60(E)
- 2004, c. 11, s. 21
- 2012, c. 20, s. 30
:::

[Previous
Version](section-30.21-20040422.html "Link to previous version of section 30.21"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Machines Installed in Educational Institutions, Libraries, Archives and Museums]{.HTitleText3} {#h-103599 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No infringement by educational
institution, etc.

- **[[30.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30.3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  educational institution or a library, archive or museum does not
  infringe copyright where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a copy of a work is made using a machine for the
    making, by reprographic reproduction, of copies of works in printed
    form;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the machine is installed by or with the approval of
    the educational institution, library, archive or museum on its
    premises for use by students, instructors or staff at the
    educational institution or by persons using the library, archive or
    museum; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} there is affixed in the prescribed manner and
    location a notice warning of infringement of copyright.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) only applies if, in respect of a
  reprographic reproduction,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the educational institution, library, archive or
    museum has entered into an agreement with a collective society that
    is authorized by copyright owners to grant licences on their behalf;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Board has, in accordance with subsection 71(2),
    fixed the royalty rates and related terms and conditions;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a tariff has been approved in accordance with
    section 70; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a collective society has filed a proposed tariff in
    accordance with section 68.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where a collective society offers to negotiate or has
  begun to negotiate an agreement referred to in paragraph (2)(a), the
  Board may, at the request of either party, order that the educational
  institution, library, archive or museum be treated as an institution
  to which subsection (1) applies, during the period specified in the
  order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Agreement with copyright owner

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where an educational institution, library, archive or
  museum has entered into an agreement with a copyright owner other than
  a collective society respecting reprographic reproduction,
  subsection (1) applies only in respect of the works of the copyright
  owner that are covered by the agreement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may, for the purposes of
  paragraph 1(c), prescribe by regulation the manner of affixing and
  location of notices and the dimensions, form and contents of notices.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 18
- [2018, c. 27, s. 285](#1039412-1121281){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039412-1121281.html"
  role="button"}
:::

#### [Libraries, Archives and Museums in Educational Institutions]{.HTitleText3} {#h-103620 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to libraries, etc. within
educational institutions

**[[30.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30.4 .sectionLabel}** For greater
certainty, the exceptions to infringement of copyright provided for
under sections 29.4 to 30.3 and 45 also apply in respect of a library,
archive or museum that forms part of an educational institution.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 18
:::

#### [Library and Archives of Canada]{.HTitleText3} {#h-103625 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Permitted acts

**[[30.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30.5 .sectionLabel}** It is not an
infringement of copyright for the Librarian and Archivist of Canada
under the [Library and Archives of Canada Act](/eng/acts/L-7.7), to

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} make a copy of a work or other subject-matter in
  taking a representative sample for the purpose of preservation under
  subsection 8(2) of that Act;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} effect the fixation of a copy of a publication, as
  defined in section 2 of that Act, that is provided by
  telecommunication in accordance with subsection 10(1) of that Act;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} make a copy of a recording, as defined in subsection
  11(2) of that Act, for the purposes of section 11 of that Act; or

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} at the time that a broadcasting undertaking, as
  defined in subsection 2(1) of the [Broadcasting
  Act](/eng/acts/B-9.01), communicates a work or other subject-matter to
  the public by telecommunication, make a copy of the work or other
  subject-matter that is included in that communication.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 18
- 2004, c. 11, s. 25
:::
