      first fixation in a sound recording or, if it is not fixed in a
      sound recording, its first performance,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a sound recording, the first
      fixation, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a communication signal, its
      broadcast; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} any additional information prescribed by
    regulation.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-42, s. 56
- 1993, c. 15, s. 6
- 1997, c. 24, s. 32
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recovery of damages

**[[56.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-56.1 .sectionLabel}** Where a person
purports to have the authority to apply for the registration of a
copyright under section 55 or 56 on behalf of another person, any damage
caused by a fraudulent or erroneous assumption of such authority is
recoverable in any court of competent jurisdiction.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 32
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration of assignment or licence

- **[[57]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-57 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Registrar of Copyrights shall register an assignment of copyright, or
  a licence granting an interest in a copyright, on being furnished with

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the original instrument or a certified copy of it,
    or other evidence satisfactory to the Registrar of the assignment or
    licence; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the fee prescribed by or determined under the
    regulations.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 1992, c. 1, s. 51\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When assignment or licence is void

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Any assignment of copyright, or any licence granting
  an interest in a copyright, shall be adjudged void against any
  subsequent assignee or licensee for valuable consideration without
  actual notice, unless the prior assignment or licence is registered in
  the manner prescribed by this Act before the registering of the
  instrument under which the subsequent assignee or licensee claims.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rectification of Register by the Court

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Federal Court may, on application of the
  Registrar of Copyrights or of any interested person, order the
  rectification of the Register of Copyrights by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the making of any entry wrongly omitted to be made
    in the Register,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the expunging of any entry wrongly made in or
    remaining on the Register, or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the correction of any error or defect in the
    Register,

  and any rectification of the Register under this subsection shall be
  retroactive from such date as the Court may order.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-42, s. 57
- 1992, c. 1, s. 51
- 1993, c. 15, s. 7
- 1997, c. 24, s. 33
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Execution of instruments

- **[[58]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-58 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  assignment of a copyright, or any licence granting an interest in a
  copyright, may be executed, subscribed or acknowledged at any place in
  a treaty country, a Rome Convention country or a WPPT country by the
  assignor, licensor or secured or hypothecary debtor, before any notary
  public, commissioner or other official, or the judge of any court, who
  is authorized by law to administer oaths or certify documents in that
  place and who also subscribes their signature and affixes to, or
  impresses on, the assignment or licence their official seal or the
  seal of the court of which they are a judge.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Execution of instruments

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any assignment of copyright, or any licence granting
  an interest in a copyright, may be executed, subscribed or
  acknowledged by the assignor, licensor or mortgagor, in any other
  foreign country before any notary public, commissioner or other
  official or the judge of any court of the foreign country, who is
  authorized to administer oaths or perform notarial acts in that
  foreign country and whose authority shall be proved by the certificate
  of a diplomatic or consular officer of Canada performing their
  functions in that foreign country.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Seals to be evidence

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The official seal or seal of the court or the
  certificate of a diplomatic or consular officer is evidence of the
  execution of the instrument, and the instrument with the seal or
  certificate affixed or attached thereto is admissible as evidence in
  any action or proceeding brought under this Act without further proof.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other testimony

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The provisions of subsections (1) and (2) shall be
  deemed to be permissive only, and the execution of any assignment of
  copyright, or any licence granting an interest in a copyright, may in
  any case be proved in accordance with the applicable rules of
  evidence.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-42, s. 58
- 1997, c. 24, s. 34
- 2012, c. 20, s. 50
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-58-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 58"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Fees]{.HTitleText2} {#h-104733 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fees regulations

**[[59]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-59 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in Council
may make regulations

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing fees, or the manner of determining fees,
  to be paid for anything required or authorized to be done in the
  administration of this Act; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the time and manner in which the fees
  must be paid.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-42, s. 59
- 1993, c. 15, s. 8
:::

## [PART VI]{.HLabel1}[Miscellaneous Provisions]{.HTitleText1} {#h-104740 .Part}

### [Substituted Right]{.HTitleText2} {#h-104741 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subsistence of substituted right

- **[[60]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-60 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  any person is immediately before January 1, 1924 entitled to any right
  in any work that is set out in column I of Schedule I, or to any
  interest in such a right, he is, as from that date, entitled to the
  substituted right set out in column II of that Schedule, or to the
  same interest in the substituted right, and to no other right or
  interest, and the substituted right shall subsist for the term for
  which it would have subsisted if this Act had been in force at the
  date when the work was made, and the work had been one entitled to
  copyright thereunder.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where author has assigned the right

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the author of any work in which any right that
  is set out in column I of Schedule I subsists on January 1, 1924 has,
  before that date, assigned the right or granted any interest therein
  for the whole term of the right, then at the date when, but for the
  passing of this Act, the right would have expired, the substituted
  right conferred by this section shall, in the absence of express
  agreement, pass to the author of the work, and any interest therein
  created before January 1, 1924 and then subsisting shall determine,
  but the person who immediately before the date at which the right
  would have expired was the owner of the right or interest is entitled
  at his option either

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on giving such notice as is hereinafter mentioned,
    to an assignment of the right or the grant of a similar interest
    therein for the remainder of the term of the right for such
    consideration as, failing agreement, may be determined by
    arbitration, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} without any assignment or grant, to continue to
    reproduce or perform the work in like manner as theretofore subject
    to the payment, if demanded by the author within three years after
    the date at which the right would have expired, of such royalties to
    the author as, failing agreement, may be determined by arbitration,
    or, where the work is incorporated in a collective work and the
    owner of the right or interest is the proprietor of that collective
    work, without any payment,

  and the notice referred to in paragraph (a) must be given not more
  than one year or less than six months before the date at which the
  right would have expired, and must be sent by registered post to the
  author, or, if he cannot with reasonable diligence be found,
  advertised in the [Canada Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
  [[author]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section,
  [[author]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes the legal representatives of a
  deceased author.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Works made before this Act in force

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Act, copyright shall not subsist in
  any work made before January 1, 1924 otherwise than under and in
  accordance with the provisions of this section.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-42, s. 60
- R.S., 1985, c. 10 (4th Supp.), s. 17(F)
- 1997, c. 24, s. 52(F)
:::

### [Clerical Errors]{.HTitleText2} {#h-104756 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Clerical errors do not invalidate

**[[61]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-61 .sectionLabel}** Clerical errors in any
instrument of record in the Copyright Office do not invalidate the
instrument, but they may be corrected under the authority of the
Registrar of Copyrights.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-42, s. 61
- 1992, c. 1, s. 52
- 1993, c. 15, s. 10
:::

### [Regulations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-104761 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

- **[[62]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-62 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of paragraph 30.01(6)(d),
    respecting measures, which may vary according to circumstances
    specified in the regulations;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of paragraph 30.02(3)(d),
    respecting measures, which may vary according to circumstances
    specified in the regulations;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the form of a notice of claimed
    infringement referred to in section 41.25 and prescribing the
    information that must be and that is not permitted to be contained
    in it;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} prescribing anything that by this Act is to be
    prescribed by regulation; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} generally for carrying out the purposes and
    provisions of this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rights saved

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make orders for altering,
  revoking or varying any order in council made under this Act, but any
  order made under this section does not affect prejudicially any rights
  or interests acquired or accrued at the date when the order comes into
  operation, and shall provide for the protection of those rights and
  interests.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-42, s. 62
- 1997, c. 24, s. 37
- 2012, c. 20, s. 51
- 2018, c. 27, s. 246
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-62-20121107.html "Link to previous version of section 62"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Industrial Designs and Topographies]{.HTitleText2} {#h-104774 .Subheading}

**[[63]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-63 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 1997, c. 24,
s. 38\]]{.Repealed}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation

- **[[64]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-64 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section and section 64.1,

  [[article]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[article]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any thing that is made by
      hand, tool or machine; ([objet]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[design]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[design]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means features of shape,
      configuration, pattern or ornament and any combination of those
      features that, in a finished article, appeal to and are judged
      solely by the eye; ([dessin]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[useful article]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[useful article]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an article that has a
      utilitarian function and includes a model of any such
      article; ([objet utilitaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[utilitarian function]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[utilitarian function]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of an
      article, means a function other than merely serving as a substrate
      or carrier for artistic or literary matter. ([fonction
      utilitaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-infringement re certain designs

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where copyright subsists in a design applied to a
  useful article or in an artistic work from which the design is derived
  and, by or under the authority of any person who owns the copyright in
  Canada or who owns the copyright elsewhere,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the article is reproduced in a quantity of more
    than fifty, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where the article is a plate, engraving or cast,
    the article is used for producing more than fifty useful articles,

  it shall not thereafter be an infringement of the copyright or the
  moral rights for anyone

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to reproduce the design of the article or a design
    not differing substantially from the design of the article by

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} making the article, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} making a drawing or other reproduction in any
      material form of the article, or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} to do with an article, drawing or reproduction that
    is made as described in paragraph (c) anything that the owner of the
    copyright has the sole right to do with the design or artistic work
    in which the copyright subsists.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (2) does not apply in respect of the
  copyright or the moral rights in an artistic work in so far as the
  work is used as or for

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a graphic or photographic representation that is
    applied to the face of an article;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a trademark or a representation thereof or a label;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} material that has a woven or knitted pattern or
    that is suitable for piece goods or surface coverings or for making
    wearing apparel;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an architectural work that is a building or a model
    of a building;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a representation of a real or fictitious being,
    event or place that is applied to an article as a feature of shape,
    configuration, pattern or ornament;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} articles that are sold as a set, unless more than
    fifty sets are made; or

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} such other work or article as may be prescribed by
    regulation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (2) and (3) apply only in respect of
  designs created after the coming into force of this subsection, and
  section 64 of this Act and the [Industrial Design Act](/eng/acts/I-9),
  as they read immediately before the coming into force of this
  subsection, as well as the rules made under them, continue to apply in
  respect of designs created before that coming into force.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-42, s. 64
- R.S., 1985, c. 10 (4th Supp.), s. 11
- 1993, c. 44, s. 68
- 1997, c. 24, s. 39
- [2014, c. 20, s. 366(E)](#1034903-1145667){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1034903-1145667.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-64-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 64"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-infringement re useful article
features

- **[[64.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-64.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The following acts do not constitute
  an infringement of the copyright or moral rights in a work:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} applying to a useful article features that are
    dictated solely by a utilitarian function of the article;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} by reference solely to a useful article, making a
    drawing or other reproduction in any material form of any features
    of the article that are dictated solely by a utilitarian function of
    the article;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} doing with a useful article having only features
    described in paragraph (a), or with a drawing or reproduction made
    as described in paragraph (b), anything that the owner of the
    copyright has the sole right to do with the work; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} using any method or principle of manufacture or
    construction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Nothing in subsection (1) affects

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the copyright, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the moral rights, if any,

  in any sound recording, cinematograph film or other contrivance by
  means of which a work may be mechanically reproduced or performed.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 10 (4th Supp.), s. 11
- 1997, c. 24, s. 40
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of Act to topographies

- **[[64.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-64.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} This Act does not apply, and shall
  be deemed never to have applied, to any topography or to any design,
  however expressed, that is intended to generate all or part of a
  topography.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Computer programs

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the incorporation of a
  computer program into an integrated circuit product or the
  incorporation of a work into such a computer program may constitute an
  infringement of the copyright or moral rights in a work.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In this section, "topography" and "integrated circuit
  product" have the same meaning as in the [Integrated Circuit
  Topography Act](/eng/acts/I-14.6).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1990, c. 37, s. 33
:::

**[[65]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-65 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 1993, c. 44,
s. 69\]]{.Repealed}

## [PART VII]{.HLabel1}[Copyright Board]{.HTitleText1} {#h-104829 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Establishment

- **[[66]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} There
  is established a Board to be known as the Copyright Board, consisting
  of not more than five members, including a Chair and a Vice-chair, to
  be appointed by the Governor in Council.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Service

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The members of the Board shall be appointed to serve
  either full-time or part-time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Chair

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Chair must be a judge, either sitting or retired,
  of a superior court.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tenure

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Each member of the Board shall hold office during
  good behaviour for a term not exceeding five years, but may be removed
  at any time by the Governor in Council for cause.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Re-appointment

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A member of the Board is eligible to be re-appointed
  once only.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A member of the Board shall not be employed in the
  public service within the meaning of the [Federal Public Sector Labour
  Relations Act](/eng/acts/P-33.3) during the member's term of office.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Members deemed public service employees

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} A full-time member of the Board, other than the
  Chair, is deemed to be employed in

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the public service for the purposes of the [Public
    Service Superannuation Act](/eng/acts/P-36); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the federal public administration for the purposes
    of any regulations made pursuant to section 9 of the [Aeronautics
    Act](/eng/acts/A-2).

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-42, s. 66
- R.S., 1985, c. 10 (1st Supp.), s. 1, c. 10 (4th Supp.), s. 12
- 2003, c. 22, s. 154(E), 224(E), 225(E)
- 2017, c. 9, s. 55
- [2018, c. 27, s. 290](#1039429-1121302){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039429-1121302.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-66-20170619.html "Link to previous version of section 66"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duties of Chair

- **[[66.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Chair shall direct the work of
  the Board and apportion its work among its members.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Absence or incapacity of Chair

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Chair is absent or incapacitated or if the
  office of Chair is vacant, the Vice-chair has all the powers and
  functions of the Chair during the absence, incapacity or vacancy.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duties of Vice-chair

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Vice-chair is the chief executive officer of the
  Board and has supervision over and direction of the Board and its
  staff.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 10 (4th Supp.), s. 12
- [2018, c. 27, s. 291(E)](#1039436-1121314){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039436-1121314.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-66.1-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 66.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Remuneration and expenses

**[[66.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66.2 .sectionLabel}** The members of the
Board shall be paid such remuneration as may be fixed by the Governor in
Council and are entitled to be paid reasonable travel and living
expenses incurred by them in the course of their duties under this Act
while absent from their ordinary place of residence.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 10 (4th Supp.), s. 12
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conflict of interest prohibited

- **[[66.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66.3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  member of the Board shall not, directly or indirectly, engage in any
  activity, have any interest in a business or accept or engage in any
  office or employment that is inconsistent with the member's duties.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Termination of conflict of interest

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a member of the Board becomes aware that he is
  in a conflict of interest contrary to subsection (1), the member
  shall, within one hundred and twenty days, terminate the conflict or
  resign.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 10 (4th Supp.), s. 12
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Staff

- **[[66.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66.4
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Such officers and employees as are
  necessary for the proper conduct of the work of the Board shall be
  appointed in accordance with the [Public Service Employment
  Act](/eng/acts/P-33.01).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The officers and employees referred to in
  subsection (1) shall be deemed to be employed in the public service
  for the purposes of the [Public Service Superannuation
  Act](/eng/acts/P-36).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Technical assistance

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Board may engage on a temporary basis the
  services of persons having technical or specialized knowledge to
  advise and assist in the performance of its duties and the Board may,
  in accordance with Treasury Board directives, fix and pay the
  remuneration and expenses of those persons.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 10 (4th Supp.), s. 12
- 2003, c. 22, s. 225(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-66.4-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 66.4"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Concluding matters after membership
expires

- **[[66.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66.5 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  member of the Board whose term expires may conclude the matters that
  the member has begun to consider.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decisions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Matters before the Board shall be decided by a
  majority of the members of the Board and the presiding member shall
  have a second vote in the case of a tie.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 10 (4th Supp.), s. 12
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fair and equitable

**[[66.501]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66.501 .sectionLabel}** The Board shall
fix royalty and levy rates and any related terms and conditions under
this Act that are fair and equitable, in consideration of

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} what would have been agreed upon between a willing
  buyer and a willing seller acting in a competitive market with all
  relevant information, at arm's length and free of external
  constraints;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the public interest;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} any regulation made under subsection 66.91(1); and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} any other criterion that the Board considers
  appropriate.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 27, s. 292](#1039438-1121322){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039438-1121322.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Informal and expeditious

**[[66.502]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66.502 .sectionLabel}** All matters
before the Board shall be dealt with as informally and expeditiously as
the circumstances and considerations of fairness permit but, in any
case, within any period or no later than any day provided for under this
Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 27, s. 292](#1039438-1121326){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039438-1121326.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}For greater certainty

**[[66.503]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66.503 .sectionLabel}** For greater
certainty, any person or entity may authorize any other person or entity
to act on their behalf in any matter before the Board.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 27, s. 292](#1039438-1121330){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039438-1121330.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Case manager

- **[[66.504]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66.504
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Chair may assign a member,
  officer or employee of the Board or a person engaged under subsection
  66.4(3) to act as a case manager of a matter before the Board.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The case manager may give any directions or make any
  orders with respect to the case management of the matter, but is not
  permitted to make a direction or order that is inconsistent with

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} this Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} regulations made under subsection 66.6(1), unless
    authorized to do so under regulations made under paragraph
    66.6(1.1)(b); or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} regulations made under paragraph 66.91(2)(a) to
    (c), unless authorized to do so under regulations made under
    paragraph 66.91(2)(d).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed direction or order of Board

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A direction given, or an order made, by a case
  manager is deemed to be a direction or order of the Board, including
  for the purposes of paragraph 28(1)(j) of the [Federal Courts
  Act](/eng/acts/F-7).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delegation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Chair may delegate his or her power under
  subsection (1) to the Vice-chair.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 27, s. 292](#1039438-1121344){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039438-1121344.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interim decisions

**[[66.51]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66.51 .sectionLabel}** The Board may, on
application, make an interim decision.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 10 (4th Supp.), s. 12
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Variation of decisions

**[[66.52]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66.52 .sectionLabel}** A decision of the
Board respecting royalties or their related terms and conditions that is
made under subsection 70(1), 71(2), 76.1(1) or 83(8) may, on
application, be varied by the Board if, in its opinion, there has been a
material change in circumstances since the decision was made.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 10 (4th Supp.), s. 12
- 1988, c. 65, s. 64
- 1997, c. 24, s. 42
- [2018, c. 27, s. 293](#1039440-1121345){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039440-1121345.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-66.52-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 66.52"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

- **[[66.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66.6
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Board may, with the approval of
  the Governor in Council, make regulations governing

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the practice and procedure in respect of the
    Board's hearings, including the number of members of the Board that
    constitutes a quorum;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the time and manner in which applications and
    notices must be made or given;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the establishment of forms for the making or giving
    of applications and notices; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the carrying out of the work of the Board, the
    management of its internal affairs and the duties of its officers
    and employees.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Case management

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} The Board may, with the approval of the Governor in
  Council, make regulations governing the case management of matters
  before the Board, including regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} governing the directions a case manager may give
    and the orders they may make; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} authorizing a case manager to give a direction or
    make an order that adapts, restricts or excludes the application of
    any provision of regulations made under subsection (1) to a matter
    or any step in a matter.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication of proposed regulations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A copy of each regulation that the Board proposes to
  make under subsection (1) or (1.1) shall be published in the [Canada
  Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) at least 60 days before the
  regulation's proposed effective date, and a reasonable opportunity
  shall be given to interested persons to make representations with
  respect to the regulation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No proposed regulation that has been published
  pursuant to subsection (2) need again be published under that
  subsection, whether or not it has been altered as a result of
  representations made with respect thereto.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 10 (4th Supp.), s. 12
- [2018, c. 27, s. 294](#1039442-1121354){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039442-1121354.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-66.6-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 66.6"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}General powers, etc.

- **[[66.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66.7
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Board has, with respect to the
  attendance, swearing and examination of witnesses, the production and
  inspection of documents, the enforcement of its decisions and other
  matters necessary or proper for the due exercise of its jurisdiction,
  all such powers, rights and privileges as are vested in a superior
  court of record.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Enforcement of decisions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any decision of the Board may, for the purposes of
  its enforcement, be made an order of the Federal Court or of any
  superior court and is enforceable in the same manner as an order
  thereof.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Procedure

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} To make a decision of the Board an order of a court,
  the usual practice and procedure of the court in such matters may be
  followed or a certified copy of the decision may be filed with the
  registrar of the court and thereupon the decision becomes an order of
  the court.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of variation of decision

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where a decision of the Board that has been made an
  order of a court is varied by a subsequent decision of the Board, the
  order of the court shall be deemed to have been varied accordingly and
  the subsequent decision may, in the same manner, be made an order of
  the court.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 10 (4th Supp.), s. 12
- 2002, c. 8, s. 131(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-66.7-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 66.7"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Distribution, publication of notices

**[[66.71]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66.71 .sectionLabel}** Independently of
any other provision of this Act relating to the distribution or
publication of information or documents by the Board, the Board may at
any time cause to be distributed or published, in any manner and on any
terms and conditions that it sees fit, any notice that it sees fit to be
distributed or published.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 43
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Studies

**[[66.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66.8 .sectionLabel}** The Board shall
conduct such studies with respect to the exercise of its powers as are
requested by the Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 10 (4th Supp.), s. 12
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report

- **[[66.9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66.9
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Board shall, not later than
  August 31 in each year, submit to the Governor in Council through the
  Minister an annual report on the Board's activities for the preceding
  year describing briefly the applications made to the Board, the
  Board's decisions and any other matter that the Board considers
  relevant.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tabling

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall cause a copy of each annual report
  to be laid before each House of Parliament on any of the first fifteen
  days on which that House is sitting after the Minister receives the
  report.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 10 (4th Supp.), s. 12
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

- **[[66.91]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66.91
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make
  regulations issuing policy directions to the Board and establishing
  general criteria to be applied by the Board or to which the Board must
  have regard

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in establishing fair and equitable royalties to be
    paid pursuant to this Act; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in rendering its decisions in any matter within its
    jurisdiction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations regarding time

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} establishing the day by which, or the period within
    which, a matter before the Board --- and any procedural step in the
    matter, whether set out in a provision of this Act or not --- must
    be completed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} establishing the minimum length of the effective
    period for the purposes of subsections 68.1(2) and 83(4);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} establishing a day for the purposes of paragraph
    73.4(b); and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} authorizing the Board or a case manager to give a
    direction or make an order that adapts, restricts or excludes the
    application of any provision of regulations made under any of
    paragraphs (a) to (c) to a matter or any step in a matter.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inconsistency or conflict

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Regulations made under subsection (2) prevail over
  regulations made under subsection 66.6(1) or (1.1) to the extent of an
  inconsistency or conflict between them.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 44
- [2018, c. 27, s. 295](#1039444-1121370){.wb-lbx

## [PART VII.1]{.HLabel1}[Collective Administration of Copyright]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1121371 .Part}

### [Collective Societies]{.HTitleText2} {#h-104939 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Filing of proposed tariffs

- **[[67]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-67 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  collective society may file a proposed tariff with the Board for the
  purpose of establishing royalties with respect to rights the
  collective society administers under section 3, 15, 18, 19 or 21.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Mandatory filing for certain royalties

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} However, a collective society shall file a proposed
  tariff with the Board for the purpose of establishing royalties
  referred to in subsection 29.7(2) or (3) or paragraph 31(2)(d).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Entering into agreements

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A collective society may enter into agreements for
  the purpose of establishing royalties with respect to rights the
  collective society administers under section 3, 15, 18, 19 or 21,
  other than royalties referred to in subsection 29.7(2) or (3) or
  paragraph 31(2)(d).

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-42, s. 67
- R.S., 1985, c. 10 (1st Supp.), s. 1, c. 10 (4th Supp.), s. 12
- 1993, c. 23, s. 3
- 1997, c. 24, s. 45
- [2018, c. 27, s. 296](#1039446-1121380){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039446-1121380.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-67-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 67"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designation of collective society ---
paragraph 19(2)(a)

**[[67.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-67.1 .sectionLabel}** On application by a
collective society, the Board may designate the collective society as
the sole collective society authorized to collect all royalties referred
to in paragraph 19(2)(a) with respect to a sound recording of a musical
work.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 10 (4th Supp.), s. 12
- 1997, c. 24, s. 45
- 2001, c. 34, s. 35(E)
- 2012, c. 20, s. 52
- [2018, c. 27, s. 296](#1039446-1121383){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039446-1121383.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-67.1-20121107.html "Link to previous version of section 67.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requests regarding repertoire

**[[67.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-67.2 .sectionLabel}** A collective society
shall answer, within a reasonable time, all reasonable requests from any
person for information about its repertoire of works, performer's
performances, sound recordings or communication signals.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 10 (4th Supp.), s. 12
- 1993, c. 23, s. 4, c. 44, ss. 71, 79
- 1997, c. 24, s. 45
- [2018, c. 27, s. 296](#1039446-1122648){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039446-1122648.html"
  role="button"}
:::

**[[67.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-67.3 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 1997, c.
24, s. 45\]]{.Repealed}

### [Tariffs]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1121387 .Subheading}

#### [Proposed Tariffs]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1121652 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Filing

**[[68]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-68 .sectionLabel}** A proposed tariff must be
filed no later than October 15 of the second calendar year before the
calendar year in which the proposed tariff is to take effect or, if a
day is established under regulations made under subsection 66.91(2), no
later than that day.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-42, s. 68
- R.S., 1985, c. 10 (4th Supp.), s. 13
- 1993, c. 23, s. 5
- 1997, c. 24, s. 45
- 2012, c. 20, s. 53
- [2018, c. 27, s. 296](#1039446-1121426){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039446-1121426.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-68-20121107.html "Link to previous version of section 68"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form and content

- **[[68.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-68.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  proposed tariff must be filed in both official languages and include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the acts to which the tariff is to apply;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the proposed royalty rates and any related terms
    and conditions; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the effective period of the proposed tariff.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minimum effective period

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A proposed tariff's effective period must be at least
  three calendar years or, if a minimum period is established under
  regulations made under subsection 66.91(2), at least that minimum
  period.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 45
- [2018, c. 27, s. 296](#1039446-1121425){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039446-1121425.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-68.1-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 68.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication and notification

**[[68.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-68.2 .sectionLabel}** The Board, in the
manner that it sees fit,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} shall publish the proposed tariff as well as a notice
  that any objection to the proposed tariff must be filed within the
  period set out in subsection 68.3(2); and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} may distribute a notice --- or cause it to be
  distributed or published, on any terms and conditions that the Board
  sees fit --- of the publication of the tariff and of the notice
  referred to in paragraph (a) to any person affected by the proposed
  tariff.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 45
- 2012, c. 20, s. 54
- [2018, c. 27, s. 296](#1039446-1121439){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039446-1121439.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-68.2-20121107.html "Link to previous version of section 68.2"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Filing of objection

- **[[68.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-68.3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  objection to a proposed tariff may be filed with the Board by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an educational institution, if the proposed tariff
    is filed for the purpose of collecting royalties referred to in
    subsection 29.7(2) or (3);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a [[retransmitter]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as
    defined in subsection 31(1), if the proposed tariff is filed for the
    purpose of collecting royalties referred to in paragraph 31(2)(d);
    or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any user, in any other case.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time for filing objection

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An objection shall be filed no later than the 30th
  day after the day on which the Board published the proposed tariff
  under paragraph 68.2(a) or, if a day is established under the
  regulations made under subsection 66.91(2), no later than that day.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copy to collective society

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Board shall provide a copy of the filed objection
  to the collective society.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 27, s. 296](#1039446-1121451){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039446-1121451.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reply to objection

- **[[68.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-68.4
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The collective society may file a
  reply to an objection with the Board.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copy to be provided

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Board shall provide a copy of the filed reply to
  the person or entity that filed the objection.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 27, s. 296](#1039446-1121458){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039446-1121458.html"
  role="button"}
:::

#### [Withdrawal or Amendment of Proposed Tariff]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1121524 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Request to withdraw or amend

**[[69]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-69 .sectionLabel}** A collective society may,
before a proposed tariff filed by it has been approved by the Board,
make an application to the Board requesting that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the proposed tariff be withdrawn; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a reference to an act set out in the proposed tariff
  be excluded from the approved tariff for all of the proposed effective
  period or, despite subsection 68.1(2), for a portion of that period.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-42, s. 69
- R.S., 1985, c. 10 (4th Supp.), s. 14
- 1993, c. 44, s. 73
- 1997, c. 24, s. 52(F)
- [2018, c. 27, s. 296](#1039446-1121478){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039446-1121478.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-69-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 69"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval by Board

- **[[69.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-69.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Board shall approve an
  application made under section 69 if it is satisfied that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the collective society has provided sufficient
    public notice of its intention to make the application;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} every person who, in respect of the proposed
    effective period, has paid royalties that would not be payable if
    the application were approved has

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} consented to the application,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} received a refund of the royalties, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} entered into an agreement under subsection
      67(3) that covers the act, repertoire or proposed effective period
      that is the subject of the application; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an application made under paragraph
    69(b) with respect to a portion of the proposed effective period,
    the application is not made for the purpose of improperly
    circumventing the required minimum effective period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}For greater certainty

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the approval of an application
  made under section 69 does not preclude the collective society from
  filing, in accordance with this Act, a proposed tariff that deals in
  whole or in part with the act, repertoire or proposed effective period
  that was the subject of the application.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 27, s. 296](#1039446-1121491){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039446-1121491.html"
  role="button"}
:::

#### [Approval of Tariffs]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1121492 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval

- **[[70]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-70 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Board shall --- within the period, if any, that is established under
  regulations made under subsection 66.91(2) --- approve the proposed
  tariff after making any alterations to the royalty rates and the
  related terms and conditions, or fixing any new related terms and
  conditions, that the Board considers appropriate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Factors --- performances of musical
  works and sound recordings

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In approving a proposed tariff for the performance in
  public or the communication to the public by telecommunication of
  performer's performances of musical works, or of sound recordings
  embodying such performer's performances, the Board shall ensure that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the tariff applies in respect of performer's
    performances and sound recordings only in the situations referred to
    in section 20;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the tariff does not, because of linguistic and
    content requirements of Canada's broadcasting policy set out in
    section 3 of the [Broadcasting Act](/eng/acts/B-9.01), place some
    users that are subject to that Act at a greater financial
    disadvantage than others; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the payment of royalties by users under section 19
    will be made in a single payment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Small cable transmission system

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Board shall fix a preferential royalty rate for
  small cable transmission systems in approving a tariff for

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the performance in public of musical works or
    dramatico-musical works, of performer's performances of such works,
    or of sound recordings embodying such works; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the communication to the public by
    telecommunication of musical works or dramatico-musical works, other
    than as described in subsection 31(2), of performer's performances
    of such works, or of sound recordings embodying such works.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Small retransmission systems

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Board shall fix a preferential royalty rate for
  small retransmission systems in approving a tariff for royalties
  referred to in paragraph 31(2)(d).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}For greater certainty

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the Board may determine, in
  respect of any tariff that it approves, the portion of the royalties
  that is to be paid to each collective society.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No discrimination

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the Board must not
  discriminate between owners of copyright on the ground of their
  nationality or residence in approving a tariff for royalties referred
  to in subsection 29.7(2) or (3) or paragraph 31(2)(d).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations defining
  "small cable transmission system" and "small retransmission system"
  for the purposes of this section.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-42, s. 70
- R.S., 1985, c. 10 (4th Supp.), s. 15
- [2018, c. 27, s. 296](#1039446-1122671){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039446-1122671.html"
  role="button"}
- [2020, c. 1, s. 33](#1237564-1253501){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1237564-1253501.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-70-20190401.html "Link to previous version of section 70"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication of approved tariff

**[[70.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-70.1 .sectionLabel}** The Board shall
publish the approved tariff in the [Canada
Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) and provide a copy of it, together
with the reasons for the Board's decision, to

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the collective society that filed the proposed
  tariff;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} every collective society that is authorized to
  collect royalties under the tariff;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} every person or entity that filed an objection under
  section 68.3; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} any other person or entity that, in the Board's
  opinion, ought to receive the copy and reasons.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 10 (4th Supp.), s. 16
- 1997, c. 24, s. 46
- [2018, c. 27, s. 296](#1039446-1121522){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039446-1121522.html"
  role="button"}
:::
