### [Fixing of Royalty Rates in Individual Cases]{.HTitleText2} {#h-105142 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to fix

- **[[71]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-71 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  collective society and a user are unable to agree on royalties to be
  paid with respect to rights under section 3, 15, 18, 19 or 21, other
  than royalties referred to in subsection 29.7(2) or (3) or paragraph
  31(2)(d), or are unable to agree on any related terms and conditions,
  the collective society or user may, after giving notice to the other
  party, apply to the Board to fix the royalty rates or any related
  terms and conditions, or both.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fixing royalties, etc.

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Board may, for a period that the Board may
  specify, fix the royalty rates or their related terms and conditions,
  or both, as the case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of subsections 70(2) and
  (3)

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsections 70(2) and (3) apply, with any necessary
  modifications, to the fixing of royalty rates or terms and conditions,
  or both, by the Board under subsection (2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}For greater certainty

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the Board may deny an
  application made under subsection (1) or any part of one.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copy of decision and reasons

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Board shall send a copy of the decision and the
  reasons for it to the collective society and the user.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
  [[user]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a user who is not otherwise authorized to do an act
    referred to in section 3, 15, 18 or 21 in respect of the works,
    performer's performances, sound recordings or communication signals
    included in a collective society's repertoire; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a user who is required to pay, in respect of sound
    recordings included in a collective society's repertoire, a royalty
    referred to in section 19 that has not otherwise been fixed or
    agreed on.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-42, s. 71
- 1997, c. 24, s. 50
- 2012, c. 20, s. 55
- [2018, c. 27, s. 296](#1039446-1121540){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039446-1121540.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-71-20121107.html "Link to previous version of section 71"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Agreement

**[[71.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-71.1 .sectionLabel}** The Board shall not
proceed with an application under section 71 in respect of any matter in
issue on which an agreement has been reached, if a notice is filed with
the Board that such an agreement has been reached.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 27, s. 296](#1039446-1121544){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039446-1121544.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Special Rules Related to Royalty Rates]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1121545 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Special royalty rates

- **[[72]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (2) and (3) apply
  despite the tariffs approved by the Board under section 70, or despite
  the royalty rates fixed under subsection 71(2), for the performance in
  public or the communication to the public by telecommunication of
  performer's performances of musical works or of sound recordings
  embodying such performer's performances.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Wireless transmission systems

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For wireless transmission systems, other than
  community systems and public transmission systems, broadcasters shall
  pay

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} \$100 on the first \$1.25 million of annual
    advertising revenues in respect of each year; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 100% of the royalties set out in the approved
    tariff or fixed under subsection 71(2) for that year on any portion
    of annual advertising revenues exceeding \$1.25 million.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Community systems

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For community systems, broadcasters shall pay
  royalties of \$100 in respect of each year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of paying royalties

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The payment of the royalties set out in
  subsection (2) or (3) fully discharges all liabilities of the system
  in question in respect of the approved tariffs or the royalties fixed
  under subsection 71(2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[advertising
  revenues]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Board may, by regulation, define "advertising
  revenues" for the purposes of subsection (2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations defining
  "community system", "public transmission system" and "wireless
  transmission system" for the purposes of this section.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 50
- 1999, c. 31, s. 61
- 2002, c. 26, s. 3
- [2018, c. 27, s. 296](#1039446-1121561){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039446-1121561.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-72-20030321.html "Link to previous version of section 72"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Radio performances in places other than
theatres

- **[[72.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  respect of public performances by means of any radio receiving set in
  any place other than a theatre that is ordinarily and regularly used
  for entertainments to which an admission charge is made, no royalties
  shall be collectable from the owner or user of the radio receiving
  set, but the Board shall, in so far as possible, provide for the
  collection in advance from radio broadcasting stations of royalties
  appropiate to the conditions produced by the provisions of this
  subsection and shall fix the amount of the same.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Expenses to be taken into account

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In fixing royalties under subsection (1), the Board
  shall take into account all expenses of collection and other outlays,
  if any, saved or savable by, for or on behalf of the owner of the
  copyright or performing right concerned or their agents, in
  consequence of that subsection.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 27, s. 296](#1039446-1121568){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039446-1121568.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Effects Related to Tariffs and Fixing of Royalty Rates]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1121569 .Subheading}

#### [Permitted Acts and Enforcement]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1121644 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of fixing royalties

**[[73]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-73 .sectionLabel}** Without prejudice to any
other remedies available to it, the collective society concerned may
collect the royalties specified in an approved tariff or fixed by the
Board under subsection 71(2) for the applicable period and, in default
of their payment, recover them in a court of competent jurisdiction.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 50
- 1999, c. 31, s. 62
- 2002, c. 26, s. 4
- [2018, c. 27, s. 296](#1039446-1121572){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039446-1121572.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-73-20030321.html "Link to previous version of section 73"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order --- compliance with terms and
conditions

**[[73.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-73.1 .sectionLabel}** Without prejudice to
any other remedies available to it, the collective society concerned may
apply to a court of competent jurisdiction for an order directing a
person to comply with any terms and conditions that are set out in an
approved tariff or that are fixed by the Board under subsection 71(2).

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 27, s. 296](#1039446-1121576){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039446-1121576.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Continuation of rights

**[[73.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-73.2 .sectionLabel}** If a proposed
tariff's effective period begins before the proposed tariff is approved,
and immediately after the expiry of the previous tariff, then, from the
start of the effective period of the proposed tariff until the earlier
of its approval and the end of its effective period,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} any person authorized under the previous tariff to do
  an act that is referred to in section 3, 15, 18 or 21 and that is
  covered by the proposed tariff may do so; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the collective society may collect the royalties in
  accordance with the previous tariff.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 27, s. 296](#1039446-1121582){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039446-1121582.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proceedings barred --- tariff

**[[73.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-73.3 .sectionLabel}** No proceedings may be
brought against a person for the infringement of a right with respect to
an act referred to in section 3, 15, 18 or 21 if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person has paid or offered to pay the royalties
  set out in an approved tariff that apply with respect to that act;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case where section 73.2 applies with respect
  to that act, the person has paid or offered to pay the royalties
  referred to in paragraph 73.2(b); or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case where no tariff has been approved with
  respect to that act and section 73.2 does not apply with respect to
  it, the person has offered to pay the royalties that are included in a
  proposed tariff and that will apply to that act once the tariff is
  approved.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 27, s. 296](#1039446-1121589){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039446-1121589.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval of request made under section 69

**[[73.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-73.4 .sectionLabel}** If the Board approves
an application made under section 69, no proceedings may be brought
against a person for the infringement of a right with respect to an act
referred to in section 3, 15, 18 or 21 if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the proposed tariff, if approved, will not apply to
  the act as a result of the Board's approval of the application; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the act occurs during the effective period set out in
  the proposed tariff and before the first anniversary of the day on
  which the collective society made its application under section 69 or,
  if a day has been established by regulations made under subsection
  66.91(2), before that day.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 27, s. 296](#1039446-1121595){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039446-1121595.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of fixing of royalties

- **[[73.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-73.5 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  any royalties or related terms and conditions are fixed under
  subsection 71(2) in respect of a person, the person may, during the
  applicable period and on paying or offering to pay the applicable
  royalties, do the act referred to in section 3, 15, 18 or 21 with
  respect to which the royalties or related terms and conditions are
  fixed, subject to those related terms and conditions and to the terms
  and conditions established by the collective society and the person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authority during application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If an application is made under subsection 71(1), a
  person in respect of whom royalties or terms and conditions may be
  fixed may, until the Board's final decision on the application, do an
  act referred to in section 3, 15, 18 or 21 to which the application
  applies if the person has offered to pay the applicable royalties in
  accordance with any applicable related terms and conditions.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 27, s. 296](#1039446-1121602){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039446-1121602.html"
  role="button"}
:::

#### [Effects of Agreement]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1121603 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No application

**[[74]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-74 .sectionLabel}** An approved tariff and
any royalty rates and related terms and conditions fixed by the Board
under subsection 71(2), as well as sections 73.2 to 73.5, do not apply
to a person in respect of the matters covered by an agreement referred
to in subsection 67(3) that applies to the person.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 50
- [2018, c. 27, s. 296](#1039446-1121606){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039446-1121606.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-74-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 74"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Claim by Copyright Owner --- Particular Royalties]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1121607 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Claims by non-members

- **[[75]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  owner of copyright who does not authorize a collective society to
  collect, for that person's benefit, royalties referred to in paragraph
  31(2)(d) is, if the work is communicated to the public by
  telecommunication during a period when an approved tariff that is
  applicable to that kind of work is effective, entitled to be paid
  those royalties by the collective society that is designated by the
  Board, of its own motion or on application, subject to the same
  conditions as those to which a person who has so authorized that
  collective society is subject.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment to non-members

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An owner of copyright who does not authorize a
  collective society to collect, for that person's benefit, royalties
  referred to in subsection 29.7(2) or (3) is, if such royalties are
  payable during a period when an approved tariff that is applicable to
  that kind of work or other subject matter is effective, entitled to be
  paid those royalties by the collective society that is designated by
  the Board, of its own motion or on application, subject to the same
  conditions as those to which a person who has so authorized that
  collective society is subject.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exclusion of other remedies

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The entitlement referred to in subsections (1)
  and (2) is the only remedy of the owner of the copyright for the
  payment of royalties for the communication, making of the copy or
  sound recording or performance in public, as the case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Measures

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Board may, for the purposes of this section,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} require a collective society to file with the Board
    information relating to payments of royalties collected by it to the
    persons who have authorized it to collect those royalties; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} by regulation, establish periods of not less than
    12 months within which the entitlements referred to in
    subsections (1) and (2) must be exercised, beginning on

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the making of the copy, in the case of royalties
      referred to in subsection 29.7(2),

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the performance in public, in the case of
      royalties referred to in subsection 29.7(3), or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the communication to the public by
      telecommunication, in the case of royalties referred to in
      paragraph 31(2)(d).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 50
- [2018, c. 27, s. 296](#1039446-1121622){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039446-1121622.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-75-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 75"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Examination of Agreements]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1121623 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
[[Commissioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[76]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of this section and section 76.1,
  [[Commissioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Commissioner of
  Competition appointed under the [Competition Act](/eng/acts/C-34).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Filing agreement with the Board

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a collective society enters into an agreement
  under subsection 67(3) with a user, either party may file a copy of
  the agreement with the Board within 15 days after it is entered into.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of section 45 of
  [Competition Act](/eng/acts/C-34)

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Section 45 of the [Competition Act](/eng/acts/C-34)
  does not apply in respect of any royalties or related terms and
  conditions arising under an agreement filed in accordance with
  subsection (2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access by Commissioner

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Commissioner may have access to the copy of an
  agreement filed in accordance with subsection (2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Request for examination

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If the Commissioner considers that an agreement filed
  in accordance with subsection (2) is contrary to the public interest,
  he or she may, after advising the parties, request that the Board
  examine it.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 50
- 2012, c. 20, s. 56
- [2018, c. 27, s. 296](#1039446-1121635){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039446-1121635.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-76-20121107.html "Link to previous version of section 76"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Examination and fixing of royalty

- **[[76.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Board shall consider a request
  by the Commissioner to examine an agreement and may, after giving the
  Commissioner and the parties to the agreement an opportunity to
  present their arguments, alter the royalties and any related terms and
  conditions arising under the agreement or fix new related terms and
  conditions.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copy of decision and reasons

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Board shall send a copy of the decision and the
  reasons for it to the parties and to the Commissioner.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 27, s. 296](#1039446-1121642){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039446-1121642.html"
  role="button"}
:::

## [PART VII.2]{.HLabel1}[Certain Applications to Board]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1121643 .Part}

### [Owners Who Cannot be Located]{.HTitleText2} {#h-105208 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Circumstances in which licence may be
issued by Board

- **[[77]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where,
  on application to the Board by a person who wishes to obtain a licence
  to use

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a published work,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a fixation of a performer's performance,

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a published sound recording, or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a fixation of a communication signal

  in which copyright subsists, the Board is satisfied that the applicant
  has made reasonable efforts to locate the owner of the copyright and
  that the owner cannot be located, the Board may issue to the applicant
  a licence to do an act mentioned in section 3, 15, 18 or 21, as the
  case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions of licence

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A licence issued under subsection (1) is
  non-exclusive and is subject to such terms and conditions as the Board
  may establish.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment to owner

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The owner of a copyright may, not later than five
  years after the expiration of a licence issued pursuant to
  subsection (1) in respect of the copyright, collect the royalties
  fixed in the licence or, in default of their payment, commence an
  action to recover them in a court of competent jurisdiction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Copyright Board may make regulations governing
  the issuance of licences under subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 50
:::

### [Compensation for Acts Done Before Recognition of Copyright or Moral Rights]{.HTitleText2} {#h-105225 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Board may determine compensation

- **[[78]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-78
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), for the
  purposes of subsections 32.4(2), 32.5(2), 33(2), 33.1(2) and 33.2(2),
  the Board may, on application by any of the parties referred to in one
  of those provisions, determine the amount of the compensation referred
  to in that provision that the Board considers reasonable, having
  regard to all the circumstances, including any judgment of a court in
  an action between the parties for the enforcement of a right mentioned
  in subsection 32.4(3) or 32.5(3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Board shall not

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} proceed with an application under subsection (1)
    where a notice is filed with the Board that an agreement regarding
    the matters in issue has been reached; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where a court action between the parties for
    enforcement of a right referred to in subsection 32.4(3) or 32.5(3),
    as the case may be, has been commenced, continue with an application
    under subsection (1) until the court action is finally concluded.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interim orders

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where the Board proceeds with an application under
  subsection (1), it may, for the purpose of avoiding serious prejudice
  to any party, make an interim order requiring a party to refrain from
  doing any act described in the order until the determination of
  compensation is made under subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 50
- 2012, c. 20, s. 57
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-78-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 78"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART VIII]{.HLabel1}[Private Copying]{.HTitleText1} {#h-105237 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-105238 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[79]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-79 .sectionLabel}** In this Part,

[[audio recording medium]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[audio recording medium]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a recording
    medium, regardless of its material form, onto which a sound
    recording may be reproduced and that is of a kind ordinarily used by
    individual consumers for that purpose, excluding any prescribed kind
    of recording medium; ([support audio]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[blank audio recording medium]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[blank audio recording medium]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an audio recording medium onto which no sounds
      have ever been fixed, and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any other prescribed audio recording
      medium; ([support audio vierge]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[collecting body]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[collecting body]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the collective
    society, or other society, association or corporation, that is
    designated as the collecting body under subsection
    83(8); ([organisme de perception]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[eligible author]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[eligible author]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an author of a musical
    work, whether created before or after the coming into force of this
    Part, that is embodied in a sound recording, whether made before or
    after the coming into force of this Part, if copyright subsists in
    Canada in that musical work; ([auteur admissible]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[eligible maker]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[eligible maker]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a maker of a sound
    recording that embodies a musical work, whether the first fixation
    of the sound recording occurred before or after the coming into
    force of this Part, if

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} both the following two conditions are met:

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the maker, at the date of that first fixation,
        if a corporation, had its headquarters in Canada or, if a
        natural person, was a Canadian citizen or permanent resident
        within the meaning of subsection 2(1) of the [Immigration and
        Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5), and

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} copyright subsists in Canada in the sound
        recording, or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the maker, at the date of that first fixation, if
      a corporation, had its headquarters in a country referred to in a
      statement published under section 85 or, if a natural person, was
      a citizen, subject or permanent resident of such a
      country; ([producteur admissible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[eligible performer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[eligible performer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the performer of a
    performer's performance of a musical work, whether it took place
    before or after the coming into force of this Part, if the
    performer's performance is embodied in a sound recording and

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} both the following two conditions are met:

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the performer was, at the date of the first
        fixation of the sound recording, a Canadian citizen or permanent
        resident within the meaning of subsection 2(1) of the
        [Immigration and Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5), and

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} copyright subsists in Canada in the
        performer's performance, or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the performer was, at the date of the first
      fixation of the sound recording, a citizen, subject or permanent
      resident of a country referred to in a statement published under
      section 85; ([artiste-interprète admissible]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

[[prescribed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[prescribed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means prescribed by regulations
    made under this Part. ([Version anglaise
    seulement]{.DefinitionOtherLangOnly})

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 50
- 2001, c. 27, s. 240
:::

### [Copying for Private Use]{.HTitleText2} {#h-105260 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where no infringement of copyright

- **[[80]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-80
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), the act
  of reproducing all or any substantial part of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a musical work embodied in a sound recording,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a performer's performance of a musical work
    embodied in a sound recording, or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a sound recording in which a musical work, or a
    performer's performance of a musical work, is embodied

  onto an audio recording medium for the private use of the person who
  makes the copy does not constitute an infringement of the copyright in
  the musical work, the performer's performance or the sound recording.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply if the act described in
  that subsection is done for the purpose of doing any of the following
  in relation to any of the things referred to in paragraphs (1)(a) to
  (c):

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} selling or renting out, or by way of trade exposing
    or offering for sale or rental;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} distributing, whether or not for the purpose of
    trade;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} communicating to the public by telecommunication;
    or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} performing, or causing to be performed, in public.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 50
:::

### [Right of Remuneration]{.HTitleText2} {#h-105276 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right of remuneration

- **[[81]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-81
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to and in accordance with
  this Part, eligible authors, eligible performers and eligible makers
  have a right to receive remuneration from manufacturers and importers
  of blank audio recording media in respect of the reproduction for
  private use of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a musical work embodied in a sound recording;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a performer's performance of a musical work
    embodied in a sound recording; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a sound recording in which a musical work, or a
    performer's performance of a musical work, is embodied.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Assignment of rights

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsections 13(4) to (7) apply, with such
  modifications as the circumstances require, in respect of the rights
  conferred by subsection (1) on eligible authors, performers and
  makers.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 50
:::

### [Levy on Blank Audio Recording Media]{.HTitleText2} {#h-105287 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability to pay levy

- **[[82]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-82 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every
  person who, for the purpose of trade, manufactures a blank audio
  recording medium in Canada or imports a blank audio recording medium
  into Canada

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is liable, subject to subsection (2) and section
    86, to pay a levy to the collecting body on selling or otherwise
    disposing of those blank audio recording media in Canada; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} shall, in accordance with subsection 83(8), keep
    statements of account of the activities referred to in paragraph
    (a), as well as of exports of those blank audio recording media, and
    shall furnish those statements to the collecting body.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No levy for exports

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No levy is payable where it is a term of the sale or
  other disposition of the blank audio recording medium that the medium
  is to be exported from Canada, and it is exported from Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 50
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Filing of proposed tariffs

- **[[83]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-83
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (14), each
  collective society may file with the Board a proposed tariff for the
  benefit of those eligible authors, eligible performers and eligible
  makers who, by assignment, grant of licence, appointment of the
  society as their agent or otherwise, authorize it to act on their
  behalf for that purpose, but no person other than a collective society
  may file any such tariff.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Filing of proposed tariff

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A proposed tariff must be filed no later than October
  15 of the second calendar year before the calendar year in which the
  proposed tariff is to take effect or, if a day is established under
  regulations made under subsection 66.91(2), no later than that day.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form and content

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A proposed tariff must be filed in both official
  languages and include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the proposed levy rates and any related terms and
    conditions; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the effective period of the proposed tariff.

  It may also include a suggestion as to whom the Board should designate
  under paragraph (8)(b).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minimum effective period

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A proposed tariff's effective period must be at least
  three calendar years or, if a minimum period is established under
  regulations made under subsection 66.91(2), at least that minimum
  period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Board, in the manner that it sees fit, shall
  publish the proposed tariff and a notice that any person or entity who
  files an objection must do so no later than the 30th day after the day
  on which the Board made the proposed tariff public or, if a day is
  established under regulations made under subsection 66.91(2), no later
  than that day.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copy of objection

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Board shall provide a copy of the filed objection
  to each collective society concerned.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reply

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Each collective society concerned may file a reply to
  an objection with the Board.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copy to objector

  [(7.1)]{.lawlabel} The Board shall provide a copy of the filed reply
  to the person or entity that filed the objection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} The Board shall, within the period that is
  established under regulations made under subsection 66.91(2),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} approve a proposed tariff, after making any
    alterations to the levy rates and the related terms and conditions,
    or fixing any new related terms and conditions, that the Board
    considers appropriate; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (8.2), designate as the
    collecting body the collective society or other society, association
    or corporation that, in the Board's opinion, will best fulfil the
    objects of sections 82, 84 and 86.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Terms and conditions

  [(8.1)]{.lawlabel} The related terms and conditions may include terms
  and conditions such as the form, content and frequency of the
  statements of account referred to in subsection 82(1), measures for
  the protection of confidential information contained in those
  statements, and the times at which the levies are payable.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designation

  [(8.2)]{.lawlabel} The Board is not obligated to designate a
  collecting body under paragraph (8)(b) if it has previously done so,
  and a designation under that paragraph remains in effect until the
  Board, under a proposed tariff or on a separate application, makes
  another designation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication of approved tariffs

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} The Board shall publish the approved tariff in the
  [Canada Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) and provide a copy of it,
  together with the reasons for the Board's decision, to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the collecting body;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} each collective society that filed a proposed
    tariff;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} every person or entity that filed an objection
    under subsection (5); and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any other person or entity that, in the Board's
    opinion, ought to receive the copy and reasons.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Continuation of rights

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} If a proposed tariff's effective period begins
  before the proposed tariff is approved and that effective period
  begins immediately after the expiry of the previous tariff, then ---
  from the start of the effective period of the proposed tariff until
  the earlier of its approval and the end of its effective period ---
  the collecting body may collect the levies in accordance with the
  previous tariff.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authors, etc., not represented by
  collective society

  [(11)]{.lawlabel} An eligible author, eligible performer or eligible
  maker who does not authorize a collective society to file a proposed
  tariff under subsection (1) is entitled, in relation to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a musical work,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a performer's performance of a musical work, or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a sound recording in which a musical work, or a
    performer's performance of a musical work, is embodied,

  as the case may be, to be paid by the collective society that is
  designated by the Board, of the Board's own motion or on application,
  the remuneration referred to in section 81 if such remuneration is
  payable during a period when an approved tariff that is applicable to
  that kind of work, performer's performance or sound recording is
  effective, subject to the same conditions as those to which a person
  who has so authorized that collective society is subject.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exclusion of other remedies

  [(12)]{.lawlabel} The entitlement referred to in subsection (11) is
  the only remedy of the eligible author, eligible performer or eligible
  maker referred to in that subsection in respect of the reproducing of
  sound recordings for private use.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of Board

  [(13)]{.lawlabel} The Board may, for the purposes of subsections (11)
  and (12),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} require a collective society to file with the Board
    information relating to payments of moneys received by the society
    pursuant to section 84 to the persons who have authorized it to file
    a tariff under subsection (1); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} by regulation, establish the periods, which shall
    not be less than twelve months, beginning when the applicable
    approved tariff ceases to be effective, within which the entitlement
    referred to in subsection (11) must be exercised.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Single proposed tariff

  [(14)]{.lawlabel} Where all the collective societies that intend to
  file a proposed tariff authorize a particular person or body to file a
  single proposed tariff on their behalf, that person or body may do so,
  and in that case this section applies, with such modifications as the
  circumstances require, in respect of that proposed tariff.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 50
- [2018, c. 27, s. 297](#1039448-1121421){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039448-1121421.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-83-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 83"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Distribution of Levies Paid]{.HTitleText2} {#h-105343 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Distribution by collecting body

**[[84]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-84 .sectionLabel}** As soon as practicable
after receiving the levies paid to it, the collecting body shall
distribute the levies to the collective societies representing eligible
authors, eligible performers and eligible makers, in the proportions
fixed by the Board.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 50
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reciprocity

- **[[85]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  the Minister is of the opinion that another country grants or has
  undertaken to grant to performers and makers of sound recordings that
  are Canadian citizens or permanent residents within the meaning of
  subsection 2(1) of the [Immigration and Refugee Protection
  Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) or, if corporations, have their headquarters in
  Canada, as the case may be, whether by treaty, convention, agreement
  or law, benefits substantially equivalent to those conferred by this
  Part, the Minister may, by a statement published in the [Canada
  Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} grant the benefits conferred by this Part to
    performers or makers of sound recordings that are citizens, subjects
    or permanent residents of or, if corporations, have their
    headquarters in that country; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} declare that that country shall, as regards those
    benefits, be treated as if it were a country to which this Part
    extends.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reciprocity

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the Minister is of the opinion that another
  country neither grants nor has undertaken to grant to performers or
  makers of sound recordings that are Canadian citizens or permanent
  residents within the meaning of subsection 2(1) of the [Immigration
  and Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) or, if corporations, have
  their headquarters in Canada, as the case may be, whether by treaty,
  convention, agreement or law, benefits substantially equivalent to
  those conferred by this Part, the Minister may, by a statement
  published in the [Canada Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} grant the benefits conferred by this Part to
    performers or makers of sound recordings that are citizens, subjects
    or permanent residents of or, if corporations, have their
    headquarters in that country, as the case may be, to the extent that
    that country grants those benefits to performers or makers of sound
    recordings that are Canadian citizens or permanent residents within
    the meaning of subsection 2(1) of the [Immigration and Refugee
    Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) or, if corporations, have their
    headquarters in Canada; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} declare that that country shall, as regards those
    benefits, be treated as if it were a country to which this Part
    extends.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of Act

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Any provision of this Act that the Minister specifies
  in a statement referred to in subsection (1) or (2)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} applies in respect of performers or makers of sound
    recordings covered by that statement, as if they were citizens of
    or, if corporations, had their headquarters in Canada; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} applies in respect of a country covered by that
    statement, as if that country were Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of Act

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to any exceptions that the Minister may
  specify in a statement referred to in subsection (1) or (2), the other
  provisions of this Act also apply in the way described in subsection
  (3).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 50
- 2001, c. 27, s. 241
:::

### [Exemption from Levy]{.HTitleText2} {#h-105365 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where no levy payable

- **[[86]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-86 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  levy is payable under this Part where the manufacturer or importer of
  a blank audio recording medium sells or otherwise disposes of it to a
  society, association or corporation that represents persons with a
  perceptual disability.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refunds

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a society, association or corporation referred
  to in subsection (1)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} purchases a blank audio recording medium in Canada
    from a person other than the manufacturer or importer, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} provides the collecting body with proof of that
    purchase, on or before June 30 in the calendar year following the
    calendar year in which the purchase was made,

  the collecting body is liable to pay forthwith to the society,
  association or corporation an amount equal to the amount of the levy
  paid in respect of the blank audio recording medium purchased.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}If registration system exists

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If regulations made under paragraph 87(a) provide for
  the registration of societies, associations or corporations that
  represent persons with a perceptual disability, subsections (1)
  and (2) shall be read as referring to societies, associations or
  corporations that are so registered.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 50
:::

### [Regulations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-105378 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[87]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-87 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in Council
may make regulations

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} respecting the exemptions and refunds provided for in
  section 86, including, without limiting the generality of the
  foregoing,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} regulations respecting procedures governing those
    exemptions and refunds,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} regulations respecting applications for those
    exemptions and refunds, and

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} regulations for the registration of societies,
    associations or corporations that represent persons with a
    perceptual disability;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} prescribing anything that by this Part is to be
  prescribed; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} generally for carrying out the purposes and
  provisions of this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 50
:::

### [Civil Remedies]{.HTitleText2} {#h-105389 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right of recovery

- **[[88]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-88
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Without prejudice to any other
  remedies available to it, the collecting body may, for the period
  specified in an approved tariff, collect the levies due to it under
  the tariff and, in default of their payment, recover them in a court
  of competent jurisdiction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Failure to pay royalties

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The court may order a person who fails to pay any
  levy due under this Part to pay an amount not exceeding five times the
  amount of the levy to the collecting body. The collecting body must
  distribute the payment in the manner set out in section 84.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order directing compliance

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where any obligation imposed by this Part is not
  complied with, the collecting body may, in addition to any other
  remedy available, apply to a court of competent jurisdiction for an
  order directing compliance with that obligation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Factors to consider

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Before making an order under subsection (2), the
  court must take into account

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} whether the person who failed to pay the levy acted
    in good faith or bad faith;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the conduct of the parties before and during the
    proceedings; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the need to deter persons from failing to pay
    levies.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 50
:::

## [PART IX]{.HLabel1}[General Provisions]{.HTitleText1} {#h-105404 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No copyright, etc., except by statute

**[[89]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-89 .sectionLabel}** No person is entitled to
copyright otherwise than under and in accordance with this Act or any
other Act of Parliament, but nothing in this section shall be construed
as abrogating any right or jurisdiction in respect of a breach of trust
or confidence.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 50
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation

**[[90]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-90 .sectionLabel}** No provision of this Act
relating to

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} copyright in performer's performances, sound
  recordings or communication signals, or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the right of performers or makers to remuneration

shall be construed as prejudicing any rights conferred by Part I or, in
and of itself, as prejudicing the amount of royalties that the Board may
fix in respect of those rights.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 50
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adherence to Berne and Rome Conventions

**[[91]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-91 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in Council
shall take such measures as are necessary to secure the adherence of
Canada to

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Convention for the Protection of Literary and
  Artistic Works concluded at Berne on September 9, 1886, as revised by
  the Paris Act of 1971; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the International Convention for the Protection of
  Performers, Producers of Phonograms and Broadcasting Organisations,
  done at Rome on October 26, 1961.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 50
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Review of Act

**[[92]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-92 .sectionLabel}** Five years after the day
on which this section comes into force and at the end of each subsequent
period of five years, a committee of the Senate, of the House of Commons
or of both Houses of Parliament is to be designated or established for
the purpose of reviewing this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 24, s. 50
- 2012, c. 20, s. 58
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-92-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 92"){.PITLink}
:::

## [SCHEDULE I]{.scheduleLabel}[(Section 60)]{.OriginatingRef}[Existing Rights]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-105427 .scheduleLabel}
:::

<figure id="105428">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
<thead>
<tr class="topdouble">
<th class="borderRight noBorderBottom top aligncenter"
scope="col">Column I</th>
<th class="noBorderBottom middle aligncenter" scope="col">Column II</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th class="borderRight borderBottom top aligncenter"
scope="col">Existing Right</th>
<th class="borderBottom top aligncenter" scope="col">Substituted
Right</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<th colspan="2" class="noBorderBottom bottom"></th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th colspan="2" class="noBorderBottom aligncenter"><em>Works other than
Dramatic and Musical Works</em></th>
</tr>

<tr>
<td class="borderRight noBorderBottom"><p>Copyright</p></td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>Copyright as defined by this Act<a
href="#fn_Ind1939_hq_8946" id="fn_Ind1939_hq_8946-ID0EBAAAEAABEA"><span
class="wb-invisible">Footnote for </span><sup>1</sup></a>.</p></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td colspan="2" class="noBorderBottom aligncenter"><em>Musical and
Dramatic Works</em></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="borderRight noBorderBottom"><p>Both copyright and performing
right</p></td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>Copyright as defined by this Act.</p></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="borderRight noBorderBottom"><p>Copyright, but not performing
right</p></td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>Copyright as defined by this Act, except
the sole right to perform the work or any substantial part thereof in
public.</p></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="borderRight"><p>Performing right, but not copyright</p></td>
<td><p>The sole right to perform the work in public, but none of the
other rights comprised in copyright as defined by this Act.</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
</figure>

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^1^](#fn_Ind1939_hq_8946-ID0EBAAAEAABEA){#fn_Ind1939_hq_8946}In
  the case of an essay, article or portion forming part of and first
  published in a review, magazine or other periodical or work of a like
  nature, the right shall be subject to any right of publishing the
  essay, article or portion in a separate form to which the author is
  entitled on January 1, 1924 or would if this Act had not been passed
  have become entitled under section 18 of An Act to amend the Law of
  Copyright, being chapter 45 of the Statutes of the United Kingdom,
  1842.
  :::

For the purposes of this Schedule the following expressions, where used
in column I thereof, have the following meanings:

[[Copyright]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in the case of a work that according
to the law in force immediately before January 1, 1924 has not been
published before that date and statutory copyright wherein depends on
publication, includes the right at common law, if any, to restrain
publication or other dealing with the work;

[[Performing right]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in the case of a work that has
not been performed in public before January 1, 1924, includes the right
at common law, if any, to restrain the performance thereof in public.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. C-30, Sch. I
- 1976-77, c. 28, s. 10
:::

## RELATED PROVISIONS {#related-provisions .scheduleTitleText}

- --- R.S., 1985, c. 10 (4th Supp.), ss. 23 to 26

  - ###### Application re moral rights {#application-re-moral-rights .MarginalNote}

    - [23]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} The rights referred to in section
      14.1 of the [Copyright Act](/eng/acts/C-42), as enacted by section
      4, subsist in respect of a work even if the work was created
      before the coming into force of section 4.

    - ###### Restriction {#restriction .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} A remedy referred to in subsection 34(1.1) of the
      [Copyright Act](/eng/acts/C-42), as enacted by section 8, may only
      be obtained where the infringement of the moral rights of the
      author occurs after the coming into force of section 8.

    - ###### Idem {#idem .MarginalNote}

      [(3)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection (1) and the repeal by
      section 3 of subsection 14(4) of the [Copyright
      Act](/eng/acts/C-42), the rights referred to in section 14.1 of
      that Act, as enacted by section 4, are not enforceable against

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a person who, on the coming into force of this
        section, is the owner of the copyright in, or holds a licence in
        relation to, a work, or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a person authorized by a person described in
        paragraph (a) to do an act mentioned in section 3 of that Act,

      in respect of any thing done during the period for which the
      person described in paragraph (a) is the owner or for which the
      licence is in force, and the rights referred to in subsection
      14(4) of that Act continue to be enforceable against a person
      described in paragraph (a) or (b) during that period as if
      subsection 14(4) of that Act were not repealed.

- --- R.S., 1985, c. 10 (4th Supp.), ss. 23 to 26

  - ###### Application re computer programs {#application-re-computer-programs .MarginalNote}

    [24]{.lawlabel} Subsection 1(2), the definition [[computer
    program]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 1(3) and section 5
    apply in respect of a computer program that was made prior to the
    day on which those provisions come into force but where, by virtue
    only of subsections 1(2) and (3) and this section, copyright
    subsists in a computer program that was made prior to May 27, 1987,
    nothing done in respect of the computer program before May 27, 1987
    shall be construed to constitute an infringement of the copyright.

- --- R.S., 1985, c. 10 (4th Supp.), ss. 23 to 26

  - ###### Making of records, perforated rolls, etc. {#making-of-records-perforated-rolls-etc. .MarginalNote}

    [25]{.lawlabel} It shall be deemed not to be an infringement of
    copyright in any musical, literary or dramatic work for any person
    to make within Canada during the six months following the coming
    into force of section 7 records, perforated rolls or other
    contrivances by means of which sounds may be reproduced and by means
    of which the work may be mechanically performed, if the person
    proves

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that before the coming into force of section 7,
      the person made such contrivances in respect of that work in
      accordance with section 29 or 30 of the [Copyright
      Act](/eng/acts/C-42) and any regulation made under section 33 of
      that Act, as they read immediately before the coming into force of
      section 7; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that the making would, had it occurred before the
      coming into force of section 7, have been deemed not to have been
      an infringement of copyright by section 29 or 30 of the [Copyright
      Act](/eng/acts/C-42), as it read immediately before the coming
      into force of section 7.

- --- R.S., 1985, c. 10 (4th Supp.), ss. 23 to 26

  - ###### Infringements before coming into force {#infringements-before-coming-into-force .MarginalNote}

    [26]{.lawlabel} Subsection 64(1) and section 64.1 of the [Copyright
    Act](/eng/acts/C-42), as enacted by section 11, apply in respect of
    any alleged infringement of copyright occurring prior to, on or
    after the day on which section 11 comes into force.

- --- 1993, c. 44, ss. 60(2), (3)

  - - ###### Application of amendments to s. 10 {#application-of-amendments-to-s.-10 .MarginalNote}

      []{.lawlabel} [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection 75(2) of this
      Act, section 10 of the [Copyright Act](/eng/acts/C-42), as enacted
      by subsection (1) of this section, applies to all photographs,
      whether made before or after the coming into force of this
      section.

- --- 1993, c. 44, ss. 60(2), (3)

  - - ###### Application of amendments to s. 11 {#application-of-amendments-to-s.-11 .MarginalNote}

      []{.lawlabel} [(3)]{.lawlabel} Except as provided by section 75 of
      this Act,

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} section 11 of the [Copyright
        Act](/eng/acts/C-42), as enacted by subsection (1) of this
        section, applies only in respect of contrivances made after the
        coming into force of this section; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} section 11 of the [Copyright
        Act](/eng/acts/C-42), as it read immediately before the coming
        into force of this section, continues to apply in respect of
        contrivances made before the coming into force of this section.

- --- 1993, c. 44, ss. 75 to 77

  - ###### Application of certain amendments {#application-of-certain-amendments .MarginalNote}

    - [75]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2),
      amendments to the [Copyright Act](/eng/acts/C-42) made by this Act
      relating to the term of copyright apply in respect of all works,
      whether made before or after the coming into force of this
      section.

    - ###### Idem {#idem-1 .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the term of the copyright in a work expires
      before the coming into force of this section, nothing in this Act
      shall be construed as extending or reviving that term.

- --- 1993, c. 44, ss. 75 to 77

  - ###### Cinematographs {#cinematographs .MarginalNote}

    - [76]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except as provided by
      subsection (2) of this section, the [Copyright
      Act](/eng/acts/C-42), as amended by this Act, applies in respect
      of all cinematographs, whether made before or after the coming
      into force of this section, subject to subsection 75(2) of this
      Act.

    - ###### Idem {#idem-2 .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} Section 10 of the [Copyright
      Act](/eng/acts/C-42), as that section read immediately before the
      coming into force of this section and in so far as it governs who
      is the author of a photograph, continues to apply in respect of
      all cinematographs made before the coming into force of this
      section that were, before the coming into force of this section,
      protected as photographs.

- --- 1993, c. 44, ss. 75 to 77

  - ###### Application of section 5 {#application-of-section-5 .MarginalNote}

    [77]{.lawlabel} Nothing in section 5 of the [Copyright
    Act](/eng/acts/C-42), as amended by this Act, confers copyright on
    works made before the coming into force of this section that did not
    qualify for copyright under section 5 of the [Copyright
    Act](/eng/acts/C-42) as it read immediately before the coming into
    force of this section.

- --- 1997, c. 24, s. 18(2)

  - - []{.lawlabel} [(2)]{.lawlabel} Section 30 of the Act, as enacted
      by subsection (1) of this section, does not apply in respect of
      collections referred to in section 30 that are published before
      the coming into force of section 30. Such collections continue to
      be governed by paragraph 27(2)(d) of the Act as it read before the
      coming into force of section 15 of this Act.

- --- 1997, c. 24, s. 20(4)

  - - []{.lawlabel} [(4)]{.lawlabel} Section 39.1 of the [Copyright
      Act](/eng/acts/C-42), as enacted by subsection (1) of this
      section, applies in respect of

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} proceedings commenced but not concluded before
        the coming into force of subsection (1) of this section; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} proceedings commenced after the coming into
        force of subsection (1) of this section.

- --- 1997, c. 24, ss. 54 to 58.1

  - [54]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, all notices published under
    subsection 5(2) of the [Copyright Act](/eng/acts/C-42) before the
    coming into force of this section are deemed to have been validly
    made and to have had force and effect in accordance with their
    terms.

- --- 1997, c. 24, ss. 54 to 58.1

  - [54.1]{.lawlabel} Section 6 of the [Copyright Act](/eng/acts/C-42)
    applies to a photograph in which copyright subsists on the date of
    the coming into force of this section, if the author is

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a natural person who is the author of the
      photograph referred to in subsection 10(2) of the [Copyright
      Act](/eng/acts/C-42), as enacted by section 7 of this Act; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the natural person referred to in subsection
      10(1.1) of the [Copyright Act](/eng/acts/C-42), as enacted by
      section 7 of this Act.

- --- 1997, c. 24, ss. 54 to 58.1

  - - [55]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} Part II of the [Copyright
      Act](/eng/acts/C-42), as enacted by section 14 of this Act, shall
      be construed as a replacement for subsections 5(3) to (6) and
      section 11 of the [Copyright Act](/eng/acts/C-42) as those
      provisions read immediately before the coming into force of
      subsection 5(3) and section 8, respectively, of this Act.

    - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The rights conferred by Part II of the [Copyright
      Act](/eng/acts/C-42), as enacted by section 14 of this Act, shall
      not be construed as diminishing the rights conferred by
      subsections 5(3) to (6) and section 11 of the [Copyright
      Act](/eng/acts/C-42) as those provisions read immediately before
      the coming into force of subsection 5(3) and section 8,
      respectively, of this Act, in relation to records, perforated
      rolls and other contrivances by means of which sounds may be
      mechanically reproduced that were made before the coming into
      force of subsection 5(3) and section 8, respectively, of this Act.

    - [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where an assignment of copyright or a grant of
      any interest therein

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} was made before the coming into force of Part
        II of the [Copyright Act](/eng/acts/C-42), as enacted by section
        14 of this Act, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} was made by the maker of a sound recording who
        was a natural person,

      subsections 14(1) and (2) of the [Copyright Act](/eng/acts/C-42)
      continue to apply in respect of that assignment or grant, with
      such modifications as the circumstances require, as if the sound
      recording was the work referred to in those subsections and the
      maker of the sound recording was its author.

- --- 1997, c. 24, ss. 54 to 58.1

  - [56]{.lawlabel} Nothing in this Act shall be construed as
    diminishing the right conferred by section 14.01 of the [Copyright
    Act](/eng/acts/C-42) as that section read immediately before the
    coming into force of section 12 of this Act.

- --- 1997, c. 24, ss. 54 to 58.1

  - [57]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the amendments to the
    [Copyright Act](/eng/acts/C-42) that eliminate references to
    "British subject" and "Her Majesty's Realms and Territories" do not
    affect any copyright or moral rights that subsisted in Canada
    immediately before the coming into force of those amendments.

- --- 1997, c. 24, ss. 54 to 58.1

  - [58]{.lawlabel} Nothing in this Act shall be construed as reviving a
    copyright that expired before the coming into force of this section.

- --- 1997, c. 24, ss. 54 to 58.1

  - [58.1]{.lawlabel} No agreement concluded before April 25, 1996 that
    assigns a right or grants an interest by licence in a right that
    would be a copyright or a right to remuneration under this Act shall
    be construed as assigning or granting any rights conferred for the
    first time by this Act, unless the agreement specifically provides
    for the assignment or grant.

- --- 1997, c. 24, ss. 62, 63

  - ###### Coming into force {#coming-into-force .MarginalNote}

    - [62]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} The following provisions come
      into force or are deemed to have come into force on June 30, 1996:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the definitions [[exclusive
        distributor]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, [[educational
        institution]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} and [[library, archive or
        museum]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 of the [Copyright
        Act](/eng/acts/C-42), as enacted by subsection 1(5) of this Act;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} section 2.6 of the [Copyright
        Act](/eng/acts/C-42), as enacted by section 2 of this Act;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} section 27.1 of the [Copyright
        Act](/eng/acts/C-42), as enacted by section 15 of this Act; and

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} section 45 of the [Copyright
        Act](/eng/acts/C-42), as enacted by section 28 of this Act.

    - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection (1), the definition
      [[exclusive distributor]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} referred to in
      paragraph (1)(a) shall be read as follows during the period
      beginning on June 30, 1996 and ending on the day that is sixty
      days after the day on which this Act is assented to:

      > ::: ReadAsText
      >
      > [[exclusive distributor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
      >
      > :   [[exclusive distributor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in
      >     relation to a book, a person who has, before or after the
      >     coming into force of this definition, been appointed in
      >     writing, by the owner or exclusive licensee of the copyright
      >     in the book in Canada, as
      >
      >     - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the only distributor of the book in
      >       Canada or any part of Canada, or
      >
      >     - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the only distributor of the book in
      >       Canada or any part of Canada in respect of a particular
      >       sector of the market. ([distributeur
      >       exclusif]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})
      > :::

    - [(3)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding paragraph (1)(d), paragraph
      45(1)(e) of the [Copyright Act](/eng/acts/C-42), as enacted by
      section 28 of this Act, shall be read as follows for the period
      beginning on June 30, 1996 and ending on the day that is sixty
      days after the day on which this Act is assented to:

      > ::: ReadAsText
      > - [(e)]{.lawlabel} to import copies, made with the consent of
      >   the owner of the copyright in the country where they were
      >   made, of any used books.
      > :::

- --- 1997, c. 24, ss. 62, 63

  - - [63]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} No exclusive distributor, within
      the meaning assigned to that expression by subsection 62(2) of
      this Act, copyright owner or exclusive licensee is entitled to a
      remedy referred to in the [Copyright Act](/eng/acts/C-42) in
      relation to an infringement referred to in subsection 27.1(1)
      or (2) of that Act, as enacted by section 15 of this Act, during
      the period beginning on June 30, 1996 and ending on the day on
      which this Act is assented to, unless

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} before the infringement occurred, notice in
        writing has been given to the person referred to in subsection
        27.1(1) or (2) of that Act, as enacted by section 15 of this
        Act, as the case may be, that

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} there is an exclusive distributor of the book
          in Canada, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} section 27.1 of that Act came into force or
          was deemed to have come into force on June 30, 1996; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an infringement referred to in
        section 27.1 of that Act, as enacted by section 15 of this Act,
        the remedy is only in relation to a book that was imported
        during that period and forms part of the inventory of the person
        referred to in section 27.1 of that Act on the day on which this
        Act is assented to.

    - [(2)]{.lawlabel} No exclusive distributor, copyright owner or
      exclusive licensee is entitled to a remedy referred to in
      subsection (1) against an educational institution, library,
      archive or museum.

    - [(3)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the expiration of the
      period referred to in subsection 62(2) of this Act does not affect
      the right of an exclusive distributor to continue, after the
      expiration of that period, legal proceedings validly commenced
      during that period.

- --- 2004, c. 11, s. 21(4)

  - ###### Application {#application .MarginalNote}

    - [21]{.lawlabel} [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) applies in respect
      of unpublished works deposited in an archive on or before
      September 1, 1999 or at any time after that date.

- --- 2012, c. 20, s. 59

  - ###### No revival of copyright in photograph {#no-revival-of-copyright-in-photograph .MarginalNote}

    - [59]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} The repeal of section 10 of the
      [Copyright Act](/eng/acts/C-42) by section 6 does not have the
      effect of reviving copyright in any photograph in which, on the
      coming into force of that section 6, copyright had expired.

    - ###### Cases where corporations were deemed to be authors {#cases-where-corporations-were-deemed-to-be-authors .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} In any case in which, immediately before the
      coming into force of section 6, a corporation is deemed, by virtue
      of subsection 10(2) of the [Copyright Act](/eng/acts/C-42) as it
      read before the coming into force of that section 6, to be the
      author of a photograph in which copyright subsists at that time,
      the copyright in that photograph continues to subsist for the term
      determined in accordance with sections 6, 6.1, 6.2, 9, 11.1 or 12
      of the [Copyright Act](/eng/acts/C-42) as if its author were the
      individual who would have been considered the author of the
      photograph apart from that subsection 10(2).

    - ###### Cases where individuals were deemed to be authors {#cases-where-individuals-were-deemed-to-be-authors .MarginalNote}

      [(3)]{.lawlabel} In any case in which an individual is deemed to
      be the author of a photograph, by virtue of subsection 10(2) of
      the [Copyright Act](/eng/acts/C-42) as it read before the coming
      into force of section 6, the individual continues, after the
      coming into force of that section 6, to be the author of that
      photograph for the purposes of the [Copyright
      Act](/eng/acts/C-42).

- --- 2012, c. 20, s. 60

  - ###### Engraving, photograph or portrait {#engraving-photograph-or-portrait .MarginalNote}

    [60]{.lawlabel} Subsection 13(2) of the [Copyright
    Act](/eng/acts/C-42), as it read immediately before the coming into
    force of section 7, continues to apply with respect to any
    engraving, photograph or portrait the plate or original of which was
    commissioned before the coming into force of that section 7.

- --- 2012, c. 20, s. 61

  - ###### No revival of copyright {#no-revival-of-copyright .MarginalNote}

    [61]{.lawlabel} Subsections 23(1) to (2) of the [Copyright
    Act](/eng/acts/C-42), as enacted by section 17, do not have the
    effect of reviving the copyright, or a right to remuneration, in any
    performer's performance or sound recording in which the copyright or
    the right to remuneration had expired on the coming into force of
    those subsections.

- --- 2012, c. 20, s. 62

  - ###### Limitation or prescription period {#limitation-or-prescription-period .MarginalNote}

    - [62]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 43.1(1) of the
      [Copyright Act](/eng/acts/C-42), as enacted by section 49, applies
      only to proceedings with respect to an act or omission that
      occurred after the coming into force of that section.

    - ###### Former limitation or prescription period continued {#former-limitation-or-prescription-period-continued .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 41(1) of the [Copyright
      Act](/eng/acts/C-42), as it read immediately before the coming
      into force of section 47, applies to proceedings with respect to
      an infringement that occurred before the coming into force of that
      section.

- --- 2015, c. 36, s. 82

  - ###### No revival of copyright {#no-revival-of-copyright-1 .MarginalNote}

    [82]{.lawlabel} Paragraph 23(1)(b) and subsection 23(1.1) of the
    [Copyright Act](/eng/acts/C-42), as enacted by section 81, do not
    have the effect of reviving the copyright, or a right to
    remuneration, in a sound recording or performer's performance fixed
    in a sound recording in which the copyright or the right to
    remuneration had expired on the coming into force of those
    provisions.

- --- 2018, c. 27, s. 299

  - ###### Paragraphs 66.501(a) and (b) {#paragraphs-66.501a-and-b .MarginalNote}

    [299]{.lawlabel} The Copyright Board is not required to consider the
    criteria set out in paragraphs 66.501(a) and (b) of the [Copyright
    Act](/eng/acts/C-42), as enacted by section 292 of this Act, in a
    matter in which it fixes royalty rates, levies or any related terms
    and conditions if the matter is commenced before the day on which
    this section comes into force.

- --- 2018, c. 27, s. 300

  - ###### Subsections 68.1(2) and 83(4) {#subsections-68.12-and-834 .MarginalNote}

    [300]{.lawlabel} Neither subsection 68.1(2) nor subsection 83(4) of
    the [Copyright Act](/eng/acts/C-42), as enacted by sections 296 and
    297 of this Act, respectively, applies with respect to a proposed
    tariff filed before the day on which this section comes into force.

- --- 2018, c. 27, s. 301

  - ###### Subsection 67.1(4) {#subsection-67.14 .MarginalNote}

    [301]{.lawlabel} Subsection 67.1(4) of the [Copyright
    Act](/eng/acts/C-42), as it read immediately before the day on which
    this section comes into force, continues to apply with respect to

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an infringement referred to in that subsection
      that occurred before that day; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the recovery of royalties to be paid under
      section 19 of that Act in relation to an act that occurred before
      that day.

- --- 2020, c. 1, s. 34

  - ###### No revival of copyright {#no-revival-of-copyright-2 .MarginalNote}

    [34]{.lawlabel} Sections 6.1, 6.2 and 11.1, paragraphs 23(1)(a)
    and (b) and subsection 23(1.1) of the [Copyright
    Act](/eng/acts/C-42), as enacted by sections 24, 26 and 29,
    respectively, do not have the effect of reviving the copyright or a
    right to remuneration in any work, performer's performance fixed in
    a sound recording or sound recording in which the copyright or the
    right to remuneration had expired on the coming into force of those
    provisions of that Act.

- --- 2022, c. 10, s. 280

  - ###### No revival of copyright {#no-revival-of-copyright-3 .MarginalNote}

    [280]{.lawlabel} Section 6, subsections 6.2(2) and 7(1) and (3) and
    section 9 of the [Copyright Act](/eng/acts/C-42), as enacted by
    sections 276 to 279, do not have the effect of reviving the
    copyright in any work in which the copyright had expired before the
    day on which sections 276 to 279 come into force.
